# Strap target product contract

Target edition: 17 October 2026. Strap is the complete boutique fitness and yoga studio system a US owner buys to sell and auto-renew memberships, intro offers and class packs, collect payments, fill classes through a branded member app and follow people from first inquiry to sustained attendance. A yoga, Pilates or boutique fitness studio with 100–500 members can replace its incumbent without running billing, messaging, payroll preparation or retention in a second studio system.

## Product target

| | |
| --- | --- |
| Category | Boutique fitness and yoga studio management software |
| Incumbents a buyer switches from | Mindbody, WellnessLiving, Vagaro, Momence, Arketa, PushPress |
| Up-and-comers it must also beat | Momence, Arketa, PushPress |
| First buyer | A US owner of a yoga, Pilates or boutique fitness studio with 100–500 members, front-desk staff and instructors, billing members in USD |

Strap owns scheduling, enrollment, memberships, payments through the studio's merchant account, POS, staff operations, member communication and retention. Its studio member pipeline is part of that category job. [Orbit](https://github.com/just-every/run-orbit) remains general CRM, [Nectar](https://github.com/just-every/run-nectar) remains general subscription billing, [Inkwell](https://github.com/just-every/run-inkwell) remains general signatures, [Pantry](https://github.com/just-every/run-pantry) remains the general ledger and [Crank](https://github.com/just-every/run-crank) remains cross-product automation. Shared infrastructure is welcome; these products keep their separate ownership.

The primary journey: configure the studio, bring members and their original records across, sell and renew their plans, book and attend classes, reconcile collections and instructor pay, then follow members through retention.

## How to read this contract

- This follows the RER contract standard.
- Core, Agent surface and Later are reported separately; the full target includes all three.
- Done means every acceptance outcome has recorded proof.
- Field proof is visible in its register and does not block Done.
- ACCEPTANCE.md, docs/CLAIMS.md and the summary counts are generated from this file.

## Door rules

- **D-1.** A member action has its corresponding STAFF-006 staff door, its separate STAFF-001 right, the same preview, policy, notice and once-only business identity, and a canonical staff-made record with actor, member, channel and before/after effect. Personal signatures, mandates, payment details and consent remain that person's act. Prove the member/staff pair and denied or revoked rights through HTTP and the 390-pixel staff view.
- **D-2.** A legacy preparation-only credential grants context and drafts; it is not a native person session and cannot supply that person's approval or consent. An authorized agent equivalent carries the server-computed classes in the Agent surface and the company's policy decides it. Legacy preparation-key refusals describe the narrow grant, not a preset for a tool or class.
- **D-3.** Member/channel consent is governed once by MEM-006 and RL-104. Campaigns, queued effects, SMS and exports use that current channel authority; one channel never authorizes another.

### Published guarantees

#### AUTH-001 — Native roles and private records

**Requirement.** Native human owners manage access and approve publication and money drafts. Members see their own balances, packs and bookings and book or cancel only their own classes; instructors see assigned rosters and check attendance only for assigned classes.

**Acceptance.**
1. Sign in through invitations as an owner, two members and two instructors; read state, submit commands and request reminder downloads across roles; unauthorized work is refused without writes.

#### AUTH-002 — Immediate revocation and origin protection

**Requirement.** Browser mutations require the configured same origin; bearer credentials cannot impersonate human sessions.

**Acceptance.**
1. Deactivate an invited member and revoke a preparation-only agent key, then reuse their sessions or credentials; attempt cross-origin and malformed commands; require refusal and unchanged records.

#### AGENT-001 — Studio drafts and company-policy decisions

**Requirement.** Agents can read studio context and prepare immutable class weeks, finite packs and balance-reminder drafts. The company policy decides an authorized agent call: internal drafts carry standard, a pack sale carries money, and an outside reminder carries external. Under the held company policy the named owner approves the exact saved draft once; changed source facts invalidate that approval. Approval keeps the human identity and time. Reminder approval exports reviewed text.

**Acceptance.**
1. With a preparation-only grant, prepare a pack and reminder and refuse use of the native human endpoint. Under the held company policy, the owner approves the saved action, downloads the exact text and cannot apply the same approval twice.

#### DATA-001 — Authoritative transactional studio records

**Requirement.** D1 is authoritative for people, capacity, credits, recorded payments and approvals. State revision and transaction fences prevent concurrent requests from overspending a credit or seat; waiting consumes no credit.

**Acceptance.**
1. Race public booking commands for one seat and one credit; inspect one confirmed booking, no negative credit balance and no duplicate committed effect.

#### BOOK-001 — Eligible credit bookings

**Requirement.** A published future class has an assigned instructor and explicit capacity. A member books only with the current waiver acknowledged and an unexpired eligible finite credit; duplicate booking is refused and the seat and credit commit together.

**Acceptance.**
1. Publish a reviewed future class, grant packs, book through the member API, attempt a duplicate and missing-waiver booking and inspect capacity and credit state.

#### BOOK-002 — Cancellation and eligible FIFO promotion

**Requirement.** Before class start an owner or the booked member cancels once, returns one spent credit and promotes the first eligible waiting member in the same transaction. Ineligible waiters cannot spend another member's credits. Configurable late-cancel rules extend this under ATT-002, and front-desk staff and managers cancel, move and restore a member's booking on the member's behalf under SCHED-014.

**Acceptance.**
1. Fill a one-seat class, add a waiter, refuse another member's cancellation, cancel the booked seat and inspect one refunded credit and the waiter's confirmed seat and single debit.

#### PACK-001 — Reviewed finite class packs

**Requirement.** The owner or an agent prepares a member-specific finite pack with credits, amount and expiry. A person confirms their own sale in the product; an agent sale carries money and the company policy decides it. Under the held company policy, approval creates the pack, owed charge and retained approval once. Class credits remain distinct from money owed. Target USD presentation and processor collection extend this foundation.

**Acceptance.**
1. Prepare a ten-credit pack, inspect no credits before approval, approve once and inspect ten credits, the exact owed cents and approval; repeat approval is refused.

#### PAY-001 — Once-only external receipt recording

**Requirement.** Owners record externally received funds against a member with positive whole cents and a unique external reference. Reusing the reference cannot reduce the balance twice. Processor collection is also required by BILL clauses; receipts are one supported payment path.

**Acceptance.**
1. Record a partial external receipt against an approved pack, repeat its reference and submit a negative amount; inspect one payment and the remaining balance.

#### WAIVER-001 — Exact current waiver acknowledgment

**Requirement.** The owner publishes immutable waiver text versions. Members acknowledge the exact current version; publishing a new version requires renewed acknowledgment before booking. An acknowledgment is a version receipt; signed waiver execution is MEM-005.

**Acceptance.**
1. Publish two waiver versions, reject booking without acknowledgment and acknowledgment of the obsolete version, acknowledge the latest and book successfully.

#### IMPORT-001 — Reviewed member CSV and reversible journal

**Requirement.** The owner previews the documented four-column Mindbody-shaped UTF-8 CSV, reviews its fingerprint and applies those exact bytes. Imports create inactive members with no invented credits or charges, retain originals and a resumable journal, and undo untouched imports while preserving later edits. Real incumbent export coverage is MOVE-001 through MOVE-006.

**Acceptance.**
1. Upload the documented CSV through import.preview, refuse altered bytes with the old fingerprint, apply and resume to completion, inspect originals, edit one member and undo; the edited member survives.

#### ARCHIVE-001 — Complete native archive and guarded restore

**Requirement.** Damaged archives and populated destinations are refused. Sessions, sign-in links, preparation-only agent keys, provider secrets and development outboxes stay installation-local. A later erasure journal fences an older archive; offline copies cannot discover later erasure unaided.

**Acceptance.**
1. Populate a studio through HTTP, export with the public CLI, restore into an empty destination and re-export equal product parts; refuse corruption, a populated destination and a pre-erasure archive with a later journal.
2. MEM-005 obligation before complete signed-document recovery ships: retain exact attributed documents, declarations, stable relationships and historical authority provenance while excluding tablet credentials, personal-copy capabilities, mail-review authority and dispatch authority.
3. Restoring history never restores former guardian access or permits new copy delivery; the native human owner must freshly review and reconnect the current installation.
4. Prove these exclusions using initialized copy records through the public archive and restore doors.

#### ERASE-001 — Unused identity erasure fence

**Requirement.** The owner can erase an unused, uninvited, non-imported identity. Its identifying data is redacted and an identity fence prevents recreation, CSV reimport and resurrection through a restore supplied with the later journal. Used-member erasure is a further target under SEC-003.

**Acceptance.**
1. Erase an unused person through person.erase, inspect absence of their email, refuse recreation and CSV reimport, and run the CLI older-backup/later-journal refusal.

#### AUDIT-001 — Retained draft approval records

**Requirement.** Class, pack and reminder approvals retain the approved draft, human approver and time. These records travel with native studio archives rather than being discarded on restore. Full staff and automation auditing is SEC-002.

**Acceptance.**
1. Approve a pack through HTTP, inspect its draft-linked approval, archive the populated studio and compare the restored approval records.

#### PUBLIC-001 — Run site and installed studio doors

**Requirement.** The run-site homepage opens /run and serves owner guides at /docs. An installed studio has a separate Lobby at /, setup and sign-in doors and private /app routes. These existing owner-approved pages keep their design and behavior.

**Acceptance.**
1. Build the run site, open its homepage, /run and /docs; open the installed Worker Lobby signed out and attempt /app before and after sign-in.
2. Confirm the public and private routes remain separate.

## Target clauses

### Studio and members

#### STUDIO-001 — US studio configuration

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-MAIL-001

**Requirement.** Owners configure the legal studio identity, location addresses, USD pricing, US phone defaults, receipt details, local time zones, business hours and daylight-saving behavior; cents and local dates are explicit everywhere. the owner saves the business's legal studio name and receipt details once, and the original location's name, US street address (state or territory code and ZIP/ZIP+4), US phone and weekly business hours on the Studio screen; each location added under STUDIO-002 has its own name, US address, US phone, IANA time zone and weekly hours. Optional values clear explicitly; location/time-zone-only saves retain the profile. US phone defaults: a phone is a US number kept as its ten digits, a leading +1 or 1 accepted and any other length refused, and shown as (718) 555-0100. Weekly hours use Monday=1 through Sunday=7 and one opening-before-closing HH:MM interval on that location's clock per open day; absent days are closed, and hours do not modify booking-window or class-availability rules. Every class, repeating series, closure and class-week row is entered and shown on its own location's clock: a weekly class keeps its local start across daylight-saving changes (a nonexistent local time is refused) while the stored instant moves, and notices, reminders, check-in windows, reports and the member and staff screens read the class's location zone, naming the zone when it differs from the studio's. Contracts, plan periods, billing days and legal windows keep the business clock, the original location's zone. Purchase receipt: every approved plan has a receipt on the member's and the owner's Packs screens naming the legal studio name, the sale location's address and phone, the exact USD cents charged (the plan price and any one-time initiation fee; no tax or processor claim), the receipt details and the sale time on the sale location's clock; it records the charge added to the balance, a payment is recorded separately, and emailed or itemized taxed receipts remain BILL-009.

**Acceptance.**
1. Configure a New York studio and a Los Angeles location; buy a USD pack, view its receipt and book across a DST transition; local times and charged cents agree across staff and member views.

#### STUDIO-002 — Locations and shared memberships

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-MAIL-001

**Requirement.** One business manages multiple studios with location-scoped staff, rooms, schedules, prices and reporting; membership eligibility explicitly permits selected or all locations and transfer does not duplicate credits. SCHED-008 obligation before this capability ships: a location closure closes only the chosen location's rooms and classes, and its impact preview and notices name that location. API-002 obligation before this capability ships: each integration key names the locations it reads and both `roster:read` and `attendance:read` return only their classes; a key created before locations existed reads only the original location, and another location's data is refused through the integration door. Both scopes inherit every API-002 rule before this capability ships. API-003 obligation before this capability ships: `attendance:read` rows are limited to the key's locations, and a key created before locations existed reads only the original location. the Studio screen's location is the original location (id `studio`); the owner adds locations, each with its own name, address, US phone, time zone and weekly hours, and no two share a name. A class, repeating series and closure belongs to one location; rooms and equipment are that location's, so one room name at two locations never conflicts, while an instructor can be in one place at a time across all of them. A plan names every location or the ones it is valid at, defaulting to its sale location; plans made before locations existed are valid at the original location only. The booking, waitlist, desk and recurring-reservation doors refuse another location with plan_location_not_eligible, and the member sees that reason. The owner changes where a plan is valid on the same pack: its credits, term and ledger are untouched, nothing is granted or copied, and a change that would strand a held future seat is refused. Each sale and receipt carries its sale location, and a charge, receipt or plan belongs to the location on it, else its class's, else its pack's, else the original; prices are the exact amounts of the sale made at a location, and per-location price lists wait for PLAN-001. A room or whole-location closure closes only that location's rooms and classes, and its preview, notices and refusals name the location. The owner grants a manager (a member identity with an email) for named locations; a manager reads the revenue and attendance reports, their downloads and their own security page for those locations only, behind a second factor, with balances recomputed from their location's rows, and nothing else; STAFF-001 carries the wider manager rights. The owner's reports consolidate every location, list each location's totals and filter by location. An integration key names the locations it reads when created, fixed for its life: roster:read and attendance:read return only their classes, another location asked for by name is refused location_not_granted, and a key made before locations existed reads only the original location.

**Acceptance.**
1. Give a member a two-location plan, book at both, deny a third location, and compare site-restricted manager and consolidated owner reports.

#### MEM-001 — Unified member history

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** Staff with permission open one member profile showing contact details, preferences, emergency contact, notes, membership state, pack usage, bookings, attendance, communications, waivers and financial history, with duplicate-merge review preserving source identities. Staff holding the member-record right (STAFF-001) create a member or walk-in profile at the desk or by phone and send the account invitation, and edit contact details and emergency contact on the member's behalf; each change records the staff member, channel and before and after values (SEC-002). ATT-001 obligation before staff notes ship: a note flagged as an alert appears in staff check-in for the roles allowed to read it, never for members or the kiosk. The governing outcome is recorded in [RULINGS.md RL-103](RULINGS.md#rl-103-unified-member-history). The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Open a migrated member, book, attend, pay and reply; see the dated timeline and ledger, merge a duplicate with review and confirm all histories remain linked.
2. As front desk, create a phoned-in member's profile, send the invitation and correct their phone number; each change appears in the timeline and the audit as staff-made, and a front-desk user without the member-record right is refused with no writes.
3. the owner sees original dated member sources and an unchanged charge/receipt ledger.
4. Duplicate confirmation links history and explicitly retires the source sign-in; it never transfers balances, booking, waiver or consent authority, and changed facts refuse confirmation.
5. Owner-only notes may be owner check-in alerts.
6. Front desk reads credits, bookings and attendance without prices, fees or payers; the charge/receipt ledger stays owner-only.
7. The owner grants front desk only named member records and separately grants creation; every product door requires a second factor, generic account-management doors stay closed, and grant changes invalidate previous sessions and pending invitation authority. Invitations record submission separately from delivery and never resend on replay. Native conversation replies retain their actual member/staff actor. An exact reviewed source-history link identifies retained and source identities and consequences before retiring source sign-in. Changed facts are refused and original provenance remains. Financial ledgers remain unchanged; the link grants no financial, booking, waiver or consent authority. Restricted notes and finances are owner-only, with explicitly scoped front-desk create, invite and contact operations.

#### MEM-002 — Households and authorized payers

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-006, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** Households retain individual participants, guardians, permissioned contacts and a shared payer; parents book and sign for authorized minors without exposing unrelated household records or granting the payer blanket staff access. Staff holding the member-record right set up a household, link a guardian and minor and change permissioned contacts on the members' behalf; making a person the payer completes only after that payer's own payment-method authorization (BILL-004) on the studio device or by secure link. a household keeps participants, guardians, permissioned contacts and one payer, and a person belongs to one household. The owner, acting as front desk with the member-record right, creates it; links a guardian only with a recorded guardianship evidence reference (the guardian needs a sign-in email); adds an existing member or a new person at the desk who has no email and cannot sign in; keeps contacts (name, US phone, relationship and the permissions pick up and be called in an emergency; a contact is never an account); and removes a guardian or participant. Each change is one canonical staff-made entry with its channel, before and after. A guardian, signed in, books, cancels and signs the current waiver for the participants of their household and reads those participants' classes, bookings, waiver status, plans and notices and the household's contacts, nothing of any other household; removing a guardian, or the guardian leaving through their own door, ends that access with their next request. Staff propose a payer and nothing changes until that person accepts, signed in: they then answer on their studio balance for the participants' plan sales, desk sales and late-cancel, no-show and late-move fees, recorded once with the previous payer, while the earlier payer's charges stay on their balance. The payer reads only the charges on their own balance and the household's names, never a participant's bookings or plans. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Create a household with two children and a payer, book each child and charge their payer, remove a guardian and verify the next request loses child access.
2. Front desk adds a third child at the desk, and a change of payer to the second guardian completes only after that guardian saves a method on the studio device; both changes are audited as staff-made.
3. APP-003 obligation before this capability ships: members manage their own household rights from their account without seeing unrelated household records; assert it at 390 pixels and desktop through the member's own door, with the owner seeing the same state and another member none of it, and through its STAFF-006 staff door.
4. LEAD-008 obligation before households ship: no feedback requests to minors and no guardian opt-in for a child; payer or guardian authority never grants access to another member’s individual feedback.
5. PLAN-010 obligation before new guest/payer doors ship: preserve guest-owned host consent, payer-owned charge authorization, studio-wide per-guest limits and the separate once-only allocation/charge ledger; STAFF-001 asserts denial and revocation at each staff door.
6. SCHED-009 obligation before its new door ships: preserve verified/revocable makeup authority, participant-owned adult delegation acceptance, the retained original absence/used visit, explicit credit expiry and one original-linked allocation; no erasure, import or staff grant may create a second makeup credit or expose unrelated child records.

#### MEM-003 — Family pricing and tuition

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-006

**Requirement.** Owners set sibling or household discounts and recurring tuition per participant with transparent stacking rules, payer allocation and end dates; removing one child recalculates future dues without rewriting past charges.

**Acceptance.**
1. Enroll two siblings, preview and collect discounted monthly tuition, withdraw one and verify the next bill and participant entitlements.
2. monthly tuition schedules are owner-managed for participants of one household with an accepted payer.
3. The owner names each participant’s monthly USD rate, credits, eligible class service and first month and end-exclusive month.
4. A sibling percentage applies to every enrolled participant only while two or more remain, rounded per participant half up to cents, without stacking.
5. The payer personally accepts each exact monthly preview before its separate participant credit packs and payer charges are created once.
6. An owner records an external receipt only after money was received; no card or bank authority is acquired.
7. Withdrawal affects only unbilled months, recalculates the next preview and leaves past charges and access untouched.
8. SEC-003 must retain anonymized tuition chronology before used-member erasure ships; MEM-002, BILL-002, BILL-004, BILL-005 and BILL-014, STAFF-001, SEC-002, SEC-003 and MOVE-009 inherit exact payer consent, original participant allocations, scoped history and no retroactive repricing before new doors ship.

#### MEM-004 — Intake forms and restricted notes

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** Studios build required and optional intake forms for signup and selected services, version questions and restrict sensitive answers by role; a changed required form prompts completion before the relevant enrollment. Staff holding the member-record right enter answers a member gives by phone, marked as staff-entered with that provenance, or launch the form on the desk tablet in locked signing mode (MEM-005); entering or reading restricted answers also needs the sensitive-notes right. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Publish an intake form, complete it on signup, change a required question and attempt a new enrollment; front desk sees permitted answers while unauthorized instructors cannot read restricted notes.
2. Front desk enters a phoned-in member's answers; they show as staff-entered in the profile and audit, and an instructor still cannot read the restricted answer.
3. the owner publishes immutable question versions for signup and all enrollments or one named class service, with a required or optional form setting and required or optional individual questions.
4. Restricted answers belong to the member and owner; a front-desk user needs explicit member-record scope plus a separately granted matching sensitive-notes scope to enter or read them.
5. Partial nonrestricted phone answers never satisfy missing required restricted answers.
6. New pack sales and every new booking, waitlist, move or restore require current applicable forms; existing paid records and bookings stay unchanged, and automatic renewal or promotion waits rather than bypassing completion.
7. Each submission retains the exact question version, actor and channel, with staff entry shown in the profile and canonical audit. Desk staff may launch a short-lived write-only handoff on a dedicated signed-out tablet: it shows no prior answers, locks private and sign-in routes, consumes once, and records tablet-member affirmation separately from native member authentication; it is not a waiver or payment signature. immutable form versions, separate audited sensitive-notes rights and signed-out one-use tablet handoffs confer no legal signing or payment authority; MEM-001, MEM-002 and MEM-005, SCHED-002, SCHED-012 and SCHED-013, BILL-002 and BILL-014, STAFF-001, SEC-002 and SEC-003 and MOVE-009 inherit exact versions, scoped reads, provenance and nontransfer of signing or payment authority before new doors ship.

#### MEM-005 — Signed waivers and enrollment policies

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-DATA-006, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1 · *Register:* R-8

**Requirement.** Signup and enrollment collect current waiver and service-specific policy signatures, guardian authority where needed, exact document version, signer attribution and completion evidence. Members receive a downloadable signed copy; expired or missing waivers block the affected service. At the desk, staff hand a tablet to a walk-in or guest in a locked signing mode that shows only that person's documents, records the device and launching staff member, and returns to a lock screen without exposing other records. The same mode presents a staff-sold membership's agreement and recurring-payment authorization for the member to review, accept and sign (BILL-014), and the signed agreement is stored on the member's profile. Staff holding the check-in right launch signing for any member at the desk or resend the member's signing link from the profile; staff never sign or acknowledge for the member. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection. Separate signing-launch scope is governed by STAFF-007; role grants remain STAFF-001. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Sign a waiver on mobile as an adult and authorized guardian, download each evidence package, replace the policy and verify re-signing is required before the affected booking; at the desk launch tablet signing for a walk-in, sign and check in, and verify the tablet returns locked with no access to other members' records; resend a member's signing link from their profile and see the launch audited as staff-made while the signature records only the member.
2. the owner explicitly opts each new immutable waiver version into personal typed affirmation.
3. Existing acknowledgments remain version receipts.
4. A typed affirmation records the exact document text, version and SHA-256, the affirmation text shown, typed name, participant, completing actor, completion time, device context and authentication channel.
5. It requires a separate explicit confirmation against the current document hash.
6. Native member completion and self-affirmed tablet completion are distinguished; tablet identity is not independently verified.
7. A guardian may affirm only for a named participant covered by an existing active recorded guardian relationship, retaining its evidence reference and authority revision. Payer status, household contact status and staff rights confer no signing authority. Ending or changing guardian authority refuses the next unauthorized request and invalidates affected pending handoffs while preserving historical attribution. BILL-014 retains owner-prepared additional membership text, personal adult signature, separate exact recurring-payment permission, readable and JSON copies, provider-hosted test card setup and immediate personal revocation. It supplements an existing accepted invoice agreement; it does not complete the original staff membership-sale, locked device signing, activation or collection target. Its evidence supplements an already accepted invoice membership and does not establish the original staff-sold membership, locked device signing, activation or collection journey inherited by MEM-005. Guardian waiver authority never supplies financial signing authority. The earlier MEM-005 typed-affirmation, scoped launch, restricted evidence, current-authority, portable-record and retention riders remain binding. An MFA-cleared native human owner separately reviews the current member-mail connection for personal-copy delivery. A person completing their own typed affirmation on the dedicated signed-out tablet may separately declare that they are an adult acting for themselves and request a private copy link to their current personal email already held by the studio; both personal controls start unchecked. Known recorded dependents are refused. Neither staff, payer, household contact nor guardian status grants this personal-copy authority. The capability permits only the exact retained typed-affirmation HTML copy, expires fifteen minutes after the request and is consumed once by a same-origin personal-device POST. A scanner GET is neutral and does not consume it; the paired shared-tablet context is refused. The capability creates no native account, session, signing delegation, payment authority or broader private access. Tablet identity and email ownership remain self-affirmed rather than independently verified. Changed participant identity, retained source evidence or current mail-review binding refuses access. Submission, provider acceptance, confirmed delivery and personal completion remain distinct; uncertain delivery never authorizes an automatic resend. Portable exports and native archives exclude copy credentials and mail-review authority, and restoration requires fresh reviewed owner reconnection before new copy dispatch. Existing native participant and currently authorized guardian evidence doors remain required. This policy approval supplies no missing execution, expiry, concurrency, resend, archive, provider-delivery, legal-authority or retention proof and removes no original MEM-005 obligation.

#### MEM-006 — Member preferences and privacy choices

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** Members control contact channels, marketing consent, preferred times and classes, accessibility requests and data-sharing choices. Staff holding the member-record right change these preferences on the member's behalf with recorded provenance: an opt-out takes effect immediately, while a marketing opt-in records the member's stated consent, channel and source, and an SMS marketing opt-in is confirmed by the member's own reply (MSG-005) before any marketing text. Marketing permission is independent of a booking or transactional notice. The governing outcome is recorded in [RULINGS.md RL-104](RULINGS.md#rl-104-member-preferences-and-privacy-choices). The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Save different transactional and marketing preferences, book a class, attempt a marketing sequence and export the preference record with its source and date.
2. Front desk records a phoned-in SMS opt-out that suppresses the next marketing text, and a verbal SMS opt-in that sends no marketing text until the member confirms by reply; both are audited as staff-made.
3. LEAD-008 obligation before feedback requests ship: keep a separate initially-off feedback choice set only by the signed-in member and fully recorded; staff may not opt in for them.
4. Marketing opt-out immediately cancels queued and future feedback requests, independently of recognition consent.
5. marketing is initially denied independently for email, SMS and in-app.
6. Staff record source/channel/stated permission; SMS opt-in is pending until the member replies to the signed callback from a uniquely matched phone.
7. STOP suppresses every matching member of a shared phone, while ambiguous positive replies confirm nobody. Booking email retains its member-owned reversal rule. Feedback starts off and remains member-only; accessibility is member/owner-only. Sharing opt-out immediately removes roster and attendance rows from existing keys. Marketing SMS quiet hours always include 20:00–10:00 in the member's recorded time zone, else the studio's, and may be stricter; they never hold operational notices or in-app marketing. MSG-005/MSG-008, API-003, LEAD-008, STAFF-001, SEC-003 and MOVE-009 inherit provenance and suppression. No outbound provider transport is claimed. Marketing permissions remain separate by channel; opt-out suppresses queued effects immediately. Staff record provenance without manufacturing consent. Positive SMS confirmation applies only to one unambiguous member; a shared-phone STOP suppresses every mapped recipient, including any later SMS mislabelled operational. The local SMS quiet-hour floor is 20:00–10:00 and stricter restrictions apply. Consent renewal does not revive a suppressed invitation. Feedback opt-in belongs to the member and accessibility requests stay member/owner-only.

### Plans and memberships

#### PLAN-001 — Recurring memberships and product catalog

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** Owners sell auto-renewing monthly, annual and fixed-term memberships, finite class packs, drop-ins and intro offers from a versioned catalog with USD prices, commitment terms and eligible services, through member checkout (BILL-002) or a staff sale at the desk or on a staff device (BILL-014); members see the complete price and renewal terms before purchase. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Publish one of each product, purchase through member checkout, sell the monthly membership to another member at the desk under BILL-014, then change the catalog price; existing contract terms and purchase evidence remain intact for both purchases.
2. PLAN-010 obligation before new guest/payer doors ship: preserve guest-owned host consent, payer-owned charge authorization, studio-wide per-guest limits and the separate once-only allocation/charge ledger; STAFF-001 asserts denial and revocation at each staff door.

#### PLAN-002 — Unlimited and period entitlements

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-AGENT-005, BASE-AGENT-007, BASE-AGENT-008 · *Rules:* D-2

**Requirement.** Memberships grant unlimited access or credits per week/month with per-service and location limits, time-of-day and weekday windows (such as off-peak memberships), rollover rules, concurrent-booking caps and the guest-pass allowance of PLAN-010. The entitlement ledger distinguishes reservation, attendance, cancellation and expiry. a membership is a member's pack sold through the reviewed pack draft (the owner or an agent prepares it, an agent sale carries money under the company policy, and the accepted price joins the external-receipt balance); nothing renews automatically, so PLAN-001 and BILL-005 carry recurring sale and renewal. A pack's plan terms are its kind (class credits, visits per week or month, or unlimited), an optional plan name, visits per period, a rollover limit from 0 to the visits per period, weekdays, a class start-time window and a cap on upcoming bookings held at once. Periods are calendar weeks from Monday 00:00 and months from the 1st 00:00 on the studio clock; a class counts in the period of its local start, and every period the plan touches, including a partial first or last one, grants the full visits. Unused visits carry into the next period only, up to the rollover limit; carried visits are used first and never carry again. Carry is projected from current holds, so a booking, waitlist promotion, desk booking or settlement, move or restore is refused when it would leave any period holding more visits than its own plus carried visits. A booking holds one visit while booked (check-in and a no-show mark it attended) and after a late cancel that keeps it under the booking's terms; an early cancel, an excused correction or a class closure returns it once. Plan hours compare the class's local start time, from inclusive and to exclusive, on the chosen weekdays (none chosen means every day); the upcoming cap counts the plan's booked classes that have not started. Visits and unlimited plans hold visits, never credits: credit adjustments and PLAN-005 changes are refused for them while expiration adjusts with a reason, and the ledger records each hold, attendance, late cancel, return and transfer with a zero credit change. Members and staff see visits left, reserved, attended, late-cancelled, returned, carried and expired by period, with eligible services and the exact expiration. Refusals name the plan: outside plan hours, plan visits used or plan upcoming limit reached. Plan-level rules (SCHED-006): a booking rule may target a plan name instead of a service; it replaces the service and studio rules for bookings that plan pays for (booking window, priority opening, cutoff, late-cancel effect and fees). A booking keeps the terms in force when made; a waitlist join takes the rule of the plan that would seat the member then. A plan sold with priority booking seats inside the priority-only window. Moves carry the same visit only into a class its plan allows, and restores reuse a kept visit or need room for a returned one (SCHED-014). Done covers the single-location studio and current sale doors: STUDIO-002 carries location limits for memberships, PLAN-010 the guest-pass allowance, PLAN-001 catalog products, member checkout and changes between memberships, and BILL-002/BILL-014 card and desk sales, each before it ships.

**Acceptance.**
1. Buy a four-visit monthly plan and an unlimited plan, cross a period boundary, book eligible and ineligible classes concurrently and inspect exactly the allowed reservations; an off-peak plan books a 2:00 pm class and is refused a 6:00 pm class.
2. SCHED-014 obligation before this capability ships: SCHED-014 membership-visit cancellation, move and restore allocation; assert the inherited behavior through its public doors.

#### PLAN-003 — Pack validity and credit history

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** Members and staff see remaining, reserved, consumed, restored and expired pack credits with eligible services and exact expiration in the studio time zone. Staff adjustments require a reason and preserve history. Service names match ignoring case and extra whitespace; an empty eligible-services list permits all classes. A class uses its explicit service or, when blank, its class name (older room-encoded titles use the class-name portion). A confirmed booking reserves one available credit, check-in marks that reservation consumed without a second debit, and cancellation returns it once. Reserved and consumed counts describe current allocations; restored is cumulative. Expiration is exclusive at the stored instant and evaluated against both booking time and class start; a browser Good through date includes that whole local day and expires at the following local midnight. Owner adjustments change available credits and/or expiration, with an exact reason, pack revision and pack-scoped retry identity; original purchase credits, price, charge and earlier events remain unchanged. Changing expiration affects unused credits; existing reservations keep their allocated credits. Every new grant, reservation, consumption, restoration and adjustment retains its actor and time. Older packs retain recorded balances and booking facts in an explicitly labelled legacy opening balance; unknown earlier events are labelled unavailable, never invented. Future plan and booking doors PLAN-001, PLAN-002, PLAN-004–012, SCHED-002, SCHED-005, SCHED-010–014 and BILL-002/014 inherit the same eligibility and credit-history obligations before shipping.

**Acceptance.**
1. Buy a pack restricted to mat classes, attempt a reformer booking, consume and return a credit, cross expiry and inspect the immutable usage and adjustment history.
2. Exercise a reasoned adjustment and its replay, refuse a changed or stale request and an unauthorized member or instructor adjustment, and confirm member and owner views agree while another member cannot read the history.

#### PLAN-004 — Intro offers and eligibility

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** immutable owner-configured offers sell finite credits for elapsed 24-hour days starting at confirmation, with the exclusive expiry on the receipt and existing service, waiver, credit-history and booking fences. The price becomes studio-balance debt; Strap collects no payment. Identity and household verification is owner-only and required before either purchase door. Additive verified same-person and household links restrict lifetime intro eligibility across all offers, including inactive accounts; they grant no sign-in, profile, booking, payer or household authority. New clients have no prior pack or attended visit across their linked person; returning clients have prior activity and no pack expiry or attended visit later than the configured inactivity cutoff. Household-limited purchases also block later linked household purchases. A changed offer, identity, source activity or purchase invalidates the 15-minute preview at confirmation. A reasoned owner exception requires the price right, has one exact sale/grant/charge identity and one canonical staff audit; ordinary staff sales use the sale right. Exception and verification reasons stay owner-only. Until STAFF-001 the native human owner alone acts for staff; delegated sale/price grant, denial and revocation remain owed there. Intro expiry alone creates no recurring terms, invoice or new pack. The existing BILL-005 native studio-invoice door separately proposes the same credit count and price at an explicitly shown calendar-month interval and first due date; only the member accepts, or declines by stopping the proposal. Provider collection remains BILL-002/BILL-014. MEM-002, MEM-006, SEC-002, SEC-003 and MOVE-009 inherit identity-link verification, restricted visibility, provenance and retained eligibility fences before their new doors ship. Owners configure intro duration, included visits, new-client or returning-client eligibility, one-per-person/household limits and explicit conversion choices. Intro expiry cannot silently enroll a member into unaccepted renewal terms. Staff holding the sale right sell an intro offer at the desk or by phone under the same eligibility checks; selling outside eligibility needs the price right and a reason, audited (SEC-002). The governing outcome is recorded in [RULINGS.md RL-105](RULINGS.md#rl-105-intro-offers-and-eligibility).

**Acceptance.**
1. Purchase a two-week intro as a new member, refuse a duplicate purchase under another account linked to the same member, use its visits and accept or decline an explicit recurring offer.
2. Front desk is refused a second intro for the same person, and a manager's eligibility exception with a reason is sold once and audited.
3. The member/staff pair follows D-1. An exact reviewed private same-person or household link restricts duplicate intro eligibility across accounts while granting no sign-in, profile, booking, payer, waiver or recurring-payment authority. Exceptions retain attribution. SEC-003 retains only the minimum eligibility linkage; an authorized reviewer sees a denial reason without another person's account. Delegated sale and price rights remain STAFF-001 obligations, and expiry creates no recurring terms.

#### PLAN-005 — Upgrades downgrades and proration preview

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-AGENT-005, BASE-AGENT-007, BASE-AGENT-008 · *Rules:* D-2, D-1

**Requirement.** Members request or make policy-permitted plan changes, or staff holding the contract-change right (STAFF-001) make them on a member's behalf from the profile, with effective date, remaining credits, prorated charge or credit and next renewal shown before confirmation. Staff approve exceptions and changed source facts invalidate a stale preview.

**Acceptance.**
1. Upgrade mid-cycle, accept the preview, downgrade at renewal and race a second change; charge and entitlements match the confirmed preview and stale approval is refused.
2. until recurring memberships ship, the plan is a member's finite pack, and the native owner acts for the contract-change right until STAFF-001.
3. A member requests a change from My packs; the owner prepares the exact preview from the packs page or the member profile, with channel and a stable request identity, audited once as staff-made under contract-change; only the member accepts it.
4. A change within the term keeps the term end and every existing allocation and takes effect at an owner-chosen future whole-second instant, which the member must accept before; credits change by the difference (refused when available credits would fall below zero), and the charge or credit is (new price − current price) × remaining seconds ÷ term seconds, counted in elapsed UTC seconds from term start to term end and rounded half-up on the magnitude to the cent.
5. A lower price or fewer credits within the term is an exception the owner approves with a recorded reason.
6. A change at renewal starts a new term at the current expiry with the full new price and credits and no proration; the ended term's unused credits expire under its own terms.
7. Packs do not renew automatically and the preview says so.
8. The charge or credit is an entry on the studio balance: Strap collects and refunds nothing and claims no payment. A preview is stale and refused when its pack's credits, price, term, credit revision or member record changed, another change was accepted, or its effective instant passed; an accepted change applies exactly once at its instant (command fence and minute sweep) and fails with no charge if the term changed or allocations would exceed the new credits. PLAN-001, PLAN-006, PLAN-007, PLAN-011, PLAN-012, BILL-005, BILL-006 and BILL-014 inherit this preview, member acceptance and once-only application for recurring plans and member-selectable catalog changes before they ship. STAFF-001 inherits contract-change grants and their denial/revocation cases; members, instructors and preparation-only agent keys are refused the staff door. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

#### PLAN-006 — Freezes and planned resumption

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** Members request a freeze with dates and reason in self-service, and authorized staff approve exceptions to a member's request; staff holding the contract-change right also freeze a membership directly on the member's behalf under PLAN-011, with no member request to approve. Freeze fees, billing-date movement, visit access and booked classes are previewed, notices are delivered and the plan resumes once on its agreed date. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Freeze a plan with future bookings, inspect the exact billing and class effects, approve, cross the dates and verify one resumption and the revised renewal charge; a staff-made freeze under PLAN-011 shows the same preview and effects.
2. APP-003 obligation before this capability ships: members request a policy-permitted freeze from their account; assert it at 390 pixels and desktop through the member's own door, with the owner seeing the same state and another member none of it, and through its STAFF-006 staff door.
3. the native owner defines freeze types and approves exact requests or direct staff freezes under the contract-change right until STAFF-001.
4. A member requests future dates and a reason; changed dates, fees or policy exceptions require the owner's recorded exception reason as well as any member acceptance below.
5. The immutable preview names the frozen pack, fee added once to its member's studio balance, every affected pack-funded or guest-funded future seat and its original funding return, revised expiry and revised native invoice date.
6. Other packs, separate makeup grants and independently authorized unpaid visits keep their doors.
7. Confirmation cancels only the listed seats with no cancellation fee and returns each original allocation once under current mail connection controls. Open-ended direct freezes resume only after staff agree an end. Earlier resumption needs a new exact preview accepted by the member, never recreates canceled bookings and charges no second fee. The scheduler crosses start and end once, including after a restart. Freeze dates are studio-clock days. Expiry and the next invoice move by the frozen calendar days at the same local time. Added days grant no new period allowance. A type may cap freezes and frozen days per rolling 365 days. A fee-bearing or changed freeze needs the member's acceptance of the exact preview. The owner declines a request with a reason, and members withdraw their own. A scheduled freeze withdrawn before it starts reverses its fee and date moves once. Each guest whose seat is cancelled is notified, and a reversed guest price credits its payer. A freeze never replaces statutory disability or relocation cancellation (PLAN-007). Current dates are studio-midnight boundaries; an ambiguous shifted local time uses its earlier occurrence and a nonexistent local time is refused. Caps apply per member and freeze type across packs; capped freezes require an end. The frozen-day cap counts actual calendar-day overlap in every rolling 365-day window; older days age out as new frozen days accrue. Paid visit and guest allowances remain anchored to the original paid term, so the extension spends only remaining allowance. Withdrawal uses an exact effects preview, leaves previously canceled bookings canceled and cancels new seats beyond the restored term with original funding returned. BILL-005 carries the native recurring-invoice agreement. BILL-002/BILL-013/BILL-014 retain provider collection and autopay obligations; PLAN-001 retains catalog and checkout, PLAN-007 and PLAN-012 retain termination and final-bill requirements, STAFF-001 retains named staff grants and revocation, and MOVE-011 retains portable records and reconnection fences before those doors ship.

#### PLAN-007 — Cancellation and remaining value

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-DATA-006, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** Members can cancel auto-renewal or submit a term-bound request in self-service, and staff holding the contract-change right terminate a membership on the member's behalf under PLAN-012 (for an emailed, phoned-in or at-the-desk cancellation); confirmation states effective date, final charge, refund policy, remaining credits and future booking disposition. Cancellation stops future renewals and reconciles every surviving entitlement. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Cancel a plan with unused credits and future reservations, approve a required exception, verify confirmation and final bill, then advance beyond renewal and confirm no further charge; a staff termination of a second plan under PLAN-012 shows the same confirmation and final bill.
2. APP-003 obligation before this capability ships: members request a policy-permitted cancellation from their account; assert it at 390 pixels and desktop through the member's own door, with the owner seeing the same state and another member none of it, and through its STAFF-006 staff door.
3. the member, the payer or a minor's guardian cancels online in one step with no reason, approval, retention gate or fee (the BILL-005 stop); access ends at the current paid term end, including freeze-added days.
4. Cancelling during a freeze is accepted: a scheduled freeze is withdrawn with its fee reversed once, and an active one ends at the termination instant.
5. Statutory cancellation (owner-configured; US default: death, disability, relocation over 25 miles, cooling-off window) takes effect on the documented request date, never at owner discretion.
6. A cooling-off cancellation records as refund due every receipt-backed payment allocated to the agreement, initiation and other fees included, and voids its unpaid invoices and financing; no funds move automatically.
7. An owner may enable only the deduction a governing rule row permits (GA: fair market value of services actually received, at most $100, document preparation excluded; FL: total price divided by the term's complete days or session count, times days elapsed or sessions rendered; CA: the pro-rata share made available for use over the term), computed from recorded days and attendance and shown in the preview and confirmation. Death, disability and relocation record the pro-rata unused prepaid amount. Each refund due is settled externally under BILL-008 and tracked to settlement by its settle-by date. Any other earlier date, on member request or by owner termination under PLAN-012, needs the owner's recorded reason, and the preview states the unused paid value and its disposition: refund due, BILL-015 credit, or forfeiture under accepted terms. The exact stale-checked preview lists effective time, existing balance, expiring credits, affected seats and final statement. At the instant, pack access ends, remaining credits stay as expired history, only that pack's future seats are cancelled with no fee and original funding returned once, guests and payers are notified, and past debt stays. Confirmation and final statement reach member and payer and join the BILL-005 evidence and BILL-008 dispute file. Only whoever terminated may withdraw before the instant while authorized; once a delegate’s authority ends, only the participant may restore access under the authority rule below. Invoices resume only on fresh member acceptance of an exact preview, never replaying old charges. Commitment, notice-period and early-termination fees apply only if accepted at sale (PLAN-001) and shown here. Cancellation authority is independent of makeup, household, booking, waiver-signing and payment-method authority. An adult participant's membership is cancelled by the participant, a payer grant, or a legal guardian grant. A payer grant names only the currently accepted agreement IDs and never covers future agreements. The participant first sees and accepts the named payer and those agreements; until then, an owner proposal shows the payer nothing and the payer cannot accept. The payer then accepts in their own signed-in account. Consent discloses that cancellation confirmations and each covered agreement's final statement and balance reach the payer; no other ledger or profile facts do. Separately, a payer whose own method or receipts fund an agreement (MEM-002, BILL-004) may stop that funding at any time in one step, without the participant's consent or a grant. No later charge reaches the payer. The participant is told only that the payer stopped and when the next payment is due. The agreement continues only if the participant accepts an exact preview to pay it; otherwise it stops at the paid term end (the BILL-005 stop). A legal guardian grant needs owner-verified evidence. The guardian accepts, and the child need not accept and cannot revoke. The grant expires at studio midnight starting the day the participant reaches the age of majority for their state on file (default 18; AL and NE 19, MS 21, not verified) or on the date of recorded emancipation evidence. A cancellation scheduled before expiry or revocation stands. Afterwards only the participant may restore it, by fresh acceptance. The participant or payer may revoke a payer grant. The owner may revoke a payer grant only through the staff door on a named party’s recorded request (the participant or the payer), with that party’s identity and the request evidence reference retained in the grant and audit, never on studio initiative; a free-text reason alone is insufficient. The guardian or owner may revoke a guardian grant. Revoking or expiry ends all further delegate access and the delegate's right to withdraw. Source digests stay opaque. MEM-002, MEM-005, BILL-004 and STAFF-001 inherit this rule. The payer's own-funding stop is a future MEM-002/BILL-004 obligation: the studio receipt command cannot fund another adult's agreement and does not implement that stop. The native owner uses the staff doors until STAFF-001. Source/stale fingerprints are opaque SHA-256 digests and preview keys are random; raw profile and ledger basis records are not exposed. Actual full-contract delivery is recorded only with an owner-entered evidence reference bound to the exact copy digest; an outbound attempt never starts the clock. A minimal owner-recorded member state joins the studio state for the longer applicable rule. Cooling-off : the window opens at the later of the member's acceptance and the recorded delivery of their copy of the full contract; without a recorded delivery it has no cutoff. It closes at the studio midnight ending the latest of: the owner's setting (at least 5 open Monday-Friday days, excluding US federal and observed holidays) and every rule for the studio's state and the member's state on file, each counted in its own days (business; studio-open excluding Sundays and holidays; eight-hour operating; calendar) including amount tiers. A studio with no open weekday counts its open non-Sunday days. Where a rule grants it, each renewal opens its own window from the renewal date (NY: 15 business days annual, 3 monthly), refunding that renewal. Where both the initial and renewal windows remain open, the INITIAL window governs and refunds the agreement’s receipt-backed payments, including earlier terms; a renewal-only refund applies only after the initial window closes. A mailed or sent request counts from its documented send date. Refund due is recorded at once with a settle-by date at the shortest applicable deadline (default 10 business days). The preview and confirmation name the closing instant and the governing rule. Shipped rule rows: NY, CA (b) and (d), GA, NJ, FL, TX, IL, MA, each citing its source, review date and verification grade (V statute, G official guide, NV unverified; an NV row has no verified date). Illinois: an agreement not accepted in person at an open studio (every online acceptance, and any whose channel is unrecorded), or sold before the studio opened, closes no earlier than seven calendar days after acceptance. The Illinois rows apply only where Illinois is the studio state or member state on file. Acceptance records online or in-person-at-an-open-studio channel and the studio opening date; an unrecorded channel is off-premises. Receipt reconciliation (ct c35 fix ruling): only an unallocated positive receipt that could fund an unallocated, unreversed charge of the agreement may hold its refund in needs-allocation. A receipt tied to a different charge, a collected booking fee or a payer’s own receipt that cannot fund those charges never blocks its refund, unpaid-invoice void or external settlement. Actual eligible receipts still require allocation; no payment is assumed.

#### PLAN-008 — Renewal disclosures and price changes

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** The studio delivers scheduled renewal, expiring-pack and price-change notices under the member's accepted terms, preserves the notice and acceptance basis, and offers the applicable cancellation or plan-change path. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Schedule an annual renewal and a price increase, deliver the notices at the configured lead time, cancel from the notice and verify no renewal collection.
2. APP-003 obligation before this capability ships: members review renewal terms and request a policy-permitted renewal change from their account; assert it at 390 pixels and desktop through the member's own door, with the owner seeing the same state and another member none of it, and through its STAFF-006 staff door.
3. Approved native notice a native human owner explicitly schedules renewal or proposed-price disclosures for an active, member-accepted studio-invoice agreement, or an expiry notice for a finite pack without an accepted renewal.
4. A configured elapsed-day lead time determines the send instant; elapsed lead time is refused.
5. Each source reserves one retained in-app notice and one Base email with the actual outcome visible.
6. Original agreement, purchase, notice and exact price-acceptance bases are retained.
7. Only the member personally accepts a future renewal price; staff open the member-owned door under contract-change and retain one canonical audit. The price applies at that exact renewal once, with the same plan entitlements, and the original acknowledgment remains verbatim. A changed due date or source after acceptance stops invoicing for owner review instead of silently reusing the amendment. Without acceptance, the original price continues. The notice links to the existing one-step stop; cancellation prevents later invoices, charges and collection. Finite-pack expiry notices create no renewal. Restore excludes installation-local disclosure dispatch authority, including at the same origin. This is studio-balance debt only, never card or bank collection. BILL-002, BILL-013 and BILL-014 inherit exact accepted price and cancellation fences before collection ships; STAFF-001 inherits the named delegated right. The native notice policy and riders were approved by ct on 24 Oct 2026; provider collection remains with BILL-002, BILL-013 and BILL-014, and STAFF-001 inherits delegated rights. Approved rider acceptance (ct 24 Oct 2026): only a native human owner schedules notices; only the affected member personally accepts the exact future price, and without acceptance the original price continues. Cancellation is immediately available from an absolute authenticated notice link, no payment authority is granted, and restored dispatch remains disconnected. The personally accepted agreement records a minimum elapsed-day price-notice period. Scheduling cannot shorten it, an actually late notice cannot support price acceptance, and a price applies only at its stated effective renewal after personal acceptance strictly before the stated accept-by instant; at expiry without acceptance the original price continues. Every affected customer receives a required email attempt regardless of booking-email preference or in-app notice state, with the exact old and proposed price, effective date, accept-by instant and cancellation link. A failed or uncertain send is retained and visible to the owner and member, never silently dropped or automatically resent.

#### PLAN-009 — Courses workshops and tuition installments

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-006, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-1

**Requirement.** Owners sell a complete course, workshop or semester with enrollment caps, deposit, installment schedule and per-session attendance; withdrawal and missed installment rules show their financial and seat effects before approval. Staff holding the sale right enroll a participant at the desk or by phone, and staff holding the contract-change right withdraw one on the member's behalf with the same effects previewed. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Enroll a household participant in a six-week course, collect the deposit and installments, record attendance and withdraw under policy; roster, installments and credited balance reconcile.
2. Front desk enrolls a second participant by phone and later withdraws them under the same preview; both actions are audited as staff-made.
3. SCHED-009 obligation before its new door ships: preserve verified/revocable makeup authority, participant-owned adult delegation acceptance, the retained original absence/used visit, explicit credit expiry and one original-linked allocation; no erasure, import or staff grant may create a second makeup credit or expose unrelated child records.
4. an owner publishes immutable session times, a cap, deposit and dated USD installments.
5. A staff sale preview names the participant and current household payer; the payer personally accepts the exact tuition before staff confirms capacity.
6. Sale and contract-change rights are checked for the participant and course location before every staff request and replay, behind existing MFA.
7. External receipts are recorded by the owner against one installment with a unique reference; no provider collection is claimed. Withdrawal and missed-installment rules: deposits and received tuition are retained; an exact withdrawal preview credits only unpaid installments whose due time is still in the future, releases remaining course seats, retains original attendance and leaves overdue debt owed. Missed installments flag owner review without automatic collection, withdrawal or outbound action. Credits here reduce this course invoice debt only and create no spendable account credit. Participant and payer receive once-only in-app outcome notices. Staff launch, personal payer acceptance, enrollment confirmation and withdrawal remain distinct; canonical staff entries retain actor, role, right, participant, channel and before and after facts. Course attendance is owner-recorded and issues no absence or makeup credit. Existing SCHED-009 authority and allocation duties remain required before a course makeup or delegated attendance door ships.

#### PLAN-010 — Guest passes and guest allowances

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001

**Requirement.** Memberships and packs can include guest passes per period (for example two a month) with eligible services, expiry, rollover and a per-guest limit such as one complimentary visit per guest per year. The studio chooses what happens when a member exceeds the allowance: refuse the booking or charge the guest drop-in price to the member's payer. Guest-pass use sits in the entitlement ledger separately from the member's own credits, and a canceled guest seat restores the pass once.

**Acceptance.**
1. Give a member a plan with two monthly guest passes, use both, attempt a third guest booking in the same period under each policy and see it refused or charged once to the payer; cross the period boundary and verify the allowance resets; another member cannot book the same guest on a guest pass beyond the per-guest limit.
2. SCHED-014 obligation before this capability ships: SCHED-014 guest-pass cancellation, move and restore allocation; assert the inherited behavior through its public doors.
3. a guest who holds a studio account signs in and explicitly authorizes a named host by exact studio email; SCHED-012 carries named guests without an account.
4. The host sees only a consenting guest's name and guest-booking facts, and no name after the guest withdraws.
5. A payer separately and revocably authorizes a per-seat cap for internal studio-balance debt only, never a stored method or provider debit; a member paying for their own online seat confirms its exact price, while a staff-made guest charge, including one to the host, also needs that payer's recorded cap.
6. Owners use the booking staff door until STAFF-001; staff launch consent but never make it, and staff cancellations record the staff member's own reason.
7. Guest allowances are calendar weeks/months on the studio clock under PLAN-002 period rules, expire with their pack, and unused passes roll into the next period only up to the chosen cap.
8. Per-guest limits apply across hosts in rolling elapsed-day windows to pass-funded holds, including existing future holds; the strictest applicable limit wins. Unavailable passes follow the sold refuse/charge policy. A charged seat adds exactly its confirmed guest price to the consenting payer's studio balance after an exact stale-checked preview; Strap claims no payment. Late-cancel, late-move and no-show fees on a guest seat are host debt under the booking's terms, never payer or guest debt, and every reversal credits the reversed charge's debtor. Guest funding has a separate ledger; any cancellation returns a pass or reverses its guest price once, a move carries the allocation, payer and confirmed price, and a restore rechecks eligibility, live guest consent and payer cap before reserving or charging again. PLAN-001, MEM-002, SCHED-012, STAFF-001 and BILL-002/BILL-004/BILL-014 inherit these funding and consent fences before their new doors ship.

#### PLAN-011 — Staff-processed freezes

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-AGENT-005, BASE-AGENT-007, BASE-AGENT-008 · *Rules:* D-2, D-1

**Requirement.** Staff holding the contract-change right (STAFF-001: held by owners and managers, grantable to front desk) freeze a member's membership on the member's behalf from the member profile in the staff web app or on a staff phone or tablet (APP-004), without any member request in self-service. A freeze takes a start date and an end date or no end date (open-ended until staff end it), and a studio-defined freeze type (for example Medical or Travel) that pre-fills its reason, freeze fee, default length and allowed start days, which staff may adjust within policy. Before confirming, staff see the same preview as PLAN-006: billing-date movement, any freeze fee, which scheduled autopays in BILL-013 are moved after the freeze, remaining credits and the future bookings affected and how they are handled under policy. On confirmation the BILL-013 schedule matches the preview exactly, autopays of a frozen membership show as scheduled after the freeze, the member receives the usual confirmation notice (MSG-001), and the action is recorded as staff-made with actor, type and comment (SEC-002). Staff can end a freeze early, and BILL-005 then resumes billing once from the revised date. Freeze types are managed by owners. An agent freeze carries standard, adding money for a fee and commitment for changed binding terms; the company policy decides execution. PLAN-006's exact member acceptance and withdrawal rules apply. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. Freeze a third member for 30 days using a "Medical" freeze type with a $10 fee; the fee is charged once, the renewal moves by 30 days, and ending the freeze early on day 20 resumes billing once from the revised date.
2. A front-desk user without the right is refused with no writes, a preparation-only agent key is refused, and all actions appear in the SEC-002 audit.

#### PLAN-012 — Staff-processed terminations and canceled terminations

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-AGENT-005, BASE-AGENT-007, BASE-AGENT-008 · *Rules:* D-2, D-1

**Requirement.** Staff holding the contract-change right (STAFF-001: held by owners and managers, grantable to front desk) terminate a member's membership on the member's behalf from the member profile in the staff web app or on a staff phone or tablet (APP-004), without any member request in self-service. A termination takes a termination date (immediately, at the end of the current billing period, or at the end of the plan's notice period or commitment term), a studio-defined termination reason code (for example Moved away or Price) and a comment. Before confirming, staff see the same preview as PLAN-007: the final charge, any early-termination fee, which scheduled autopays in BILL-013 are removed by the termination, remaining credits and the future bookings affected and how they are handled under policy. On confirmation the BILL-013 schedule matches the preview exactly, the member receives the usual confirmation notice (MSG-001), and the action is recorded as staff-made with actor, reason code and comment (SEC-002). On the termination date access ends, exactly one final charge is collected where due, no later renewal is created, and future bookings past the date are canceled with credits handled under the cancellation policy. A scheduled termination can be canceled until its effective date, which restores the removed future autopays exactly once with no period charged twice or dropped. Termination reason codes are managed by owners; termination reason codes feed churn by reason in RPT-003 and scheduled terminations appear in RPT-007. An agent termination carries commitment and money when it changes charges; the company policy decides execution. PLAN-007's ruled cancellation and refund semantics apply: only the terminator may withdraw before the instant, and future invoices require fresh member acceptance of an exact preview. The member/staff pair follows D-1.

**Acceptance.**
1. As front desk holding the contract-change right on a 390-pixel staff view, terminate a monthly member at the end of a 30-day notice period with reason code "Moved away"; the preview lists the one remaining charge and the removed later autopays, and the BILL-013 schedule then matches it.
2. Advance past the date: exactly one final charge, no later renewal, access ends on the date, and future bookings past it are canceled with credits handled under policy.
3. On a second member, schedule a termination and then cancel it before the date; the original autopay schedule returns with no period charged twice or dropped.
4. RPT-003 churn by reason shows "Moved away".
5. A front-desk user without the right is refused with no writes, a preparation-only agent key is refused, and all actions appear in the SEC-002 audit.

### Scheduling and attendance

#### SCHED-001 — Recurring timetable and series edits

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-AGENT-005, BASE-AGENT-007, BASE-AGENT-008 · *Rules:* D-2

**Requirement.** Owners publish repeating schedules with local times, instructor, location, room and capacity; edit one occurrence or this-and-future occurrences with a preview of existing bookings and notifications. DST keeps the intended local hour. SCHED-008 obligation before this capability ships: cancelling an occurrence of a series asks for the scope (this occurrence, this and following, or the whole series) and previews and applies the closure effects to exactly the chosen occurrences. the native owner publishes a finite repeating class timetable from an immutable draft, with a first date, inclusive last date, whole 1–52-week interval, 5–600-minute elapsed duration and at most 104 occurrences. Each occurrence retains the studio location and IANA time zone, instructor, service, room, equipment, preparation/cleanup buffers, total capacity and online capacity. The local start hour survives DST; nonexistent local times are refused, and a repeated local hour resolves to the exact UTC instant frozen in the draft. Agent timetable changes carry standard and add external when they notify members; the company policy decides publication. An edit selects this occurrence or this and following occurrences in the original occurrence order, excluding started or canceled occurrences. A date change shifts the selected occurrences by the same number of local calendar days; time, instructor, room, service, capacity and equipment changes are reviewed before approval. The preview lists before/after classes, every affected booking, spot allocation and exact notice, with unchanged credits and no money movement. Existing booking identities, credit allocations, cancellation terms and online/desk-held pools remain; incompatible capacity, enabled spots, credit eligibility/expiry or a live offer deadline beyond the proposed start refuse the edit. Changes to source facts invalidate the preview, and resource conflicts, buffers, instructor availability and closures are rechecked under the shared transaction fence at approval. Each affected booking gets one in-app notice and one configured Base email reserved after commit with honest provider outcome labels; unchanged occurrences notify nobody, and approval replay never repeats an effect. Cancellation requires an explicit scope: this occurrence, this and following, or the whole remaining series.
