MEM-005 obligation before wider staff signing rights ship: grant, deny and revoke the separate member-record and check-in scopes for each named participant and location, require MFA, and recheck authority on launch, resend and pending handoff replay. Staff start the personal act but never sign for the participant, and launch rights grant no completed-evidence access. Preserve the corresponding HTTP and 390-pixel staff acceptance cases. The native owner grants explicit named rights within the recorded role, participant and location scope. A role’s permitted-right list defines eligibility to receive a grant and grants nothing by itself; unchecked rights confer no authority. Privileged staff require MFA, and every read, download, preview, confirmation and committed replay rechecks current authority. Privacy fulfillment and generic native identity administration remain owner-only. This does not remove established scoped member-record invitations, separately granted sensitive-intake access, assigned-instructor access, STUDIO-002 manager reports or SEC-004 finance-report access. Ungranted restricted information remains denied. Booking, override, fee-waiver, check-in, sale, price, membership-sale, collection, refund, contract-change, member-record, exports, campaigns and staff-pay rights remain distinct. Staff may start a member or payer action but never supply that person’s signature, financial authorization, mandate, payment details or personal consent. MEM-005 launch rights grant no completed-evidence access. Canonical migration retains exact legacy scope without widening authority. Restored grants are historical product records; source sessions and MFA do not transfer, and fresh native authentication and current rights remain required. Remaining STAFF-001 proof: complete the original role and two-location workflow matrix, including all permitted, denied and revoked sale, price, refund, collection, contract-change, household, guest, makeup, signing, campaign, export and staff-pay doors. Prove migration from populated legacy grants without widened scope. Complete every inherited STAFF-006 delegated staff pair and required 390-pixel staff interaction, canonical audit and personal authorization step. Native recurring-invoice preparation does not satisfy the original membership sale and autopay journey. Preserve all existing SCHED-005, SCHED-014, MEM-005, PLAN-010, SCHED-009 and LEAD-008 receiving duties.

#### STAFF-002 — Instructor rates and teaching pay

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001

**Requirement.** Owners version flat-class, per-appointment, hourly, per-attendee, tiered and substitute pay rules with effective dates and cancellation treatment. Pay calculations use corrected attendance and agreed minimums, and staff can inspect their own breakdown. Semantics for studio (ct 20 October): native owners publish immutable future-effective UTC rate versions; instructor+service, instructor-only, service-only, then studio-wide rules take precedence, and the latest applicable effective instant wins within a scope. Services match exact trimmed names. Flat class rates, elapsed-time hourly rates rounded once half up to cents, per-attendee rates and marginal inclusive attendance tiers have explicit minimums. Canceled classes use no pay, normal calculated pay or an explicit fixed amount; a rule created after cancellation cannot reprice it. Ended or canceled classes alone enter earned compensation. Current authoritative attended booking records, including corrections, determine attendance; unconfigured rates produce unavailable totals. Instructor breakdowns include only their assigned classes and applicable rule versions. Only the owner records additive signed-cent adjustments with a reason and a stable request identity; changed replay is refused, original rules and calculation basis remain. Configured appointment and substitute terms are retained without inventing appointments or approved substitution provenance. SCHED-002 inherits per-appointment pay, SCHED-004 inherits approved-substitution rate selection and roster/pay transfer, and STAFF-003 inherits time-clock/hour corrections before those capabilities ship; STAFF-001 inherits payroll grants and revocation. STAFF-004 alone executes the payroll handoff.

**Acceptance.**
1. Teach full, low-attendance, canceled and substituted classes and a private appointment under different rules; correct one attendance record and compare the pay report with the versioned calculation and authorized adjustment.

#### STAFF-003 — Time clock and approved hours

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** Staff clock in and out with location and job, record breaks and submit corrections; managers approve hours and overtime exceptions before payroll export, with original and corrected entries retained.

**Acceptance.**
1. Clock two shifts, miss a clock-out, request a correction and approve it; export approved hours only and verify another location's manager cannot approve them.
2. Semantics under the ct approval of 23 October 2026: active instructors and native owners clock only their own shifts, with location, job and server-recorded times; breaks and raw clock events remain immutable.
3. Staff request reasoned corrections, including a missing clock-out, with original and proposed entries retained.
4. Another native owner or a location manager with an explicit time-approval grant approves an exact current shift revision; self-approval is refused.
5. The manager grant is restricted to assigned locations, requires a second factor and is checked again before request replay.
6. A changed location scope or revoked approval grant refuses the next request without writes.
7. Report-only managers cannot approve.
8. Incomplete shifts and pending corrections are excluded from export; the latest approved basis alone enters the owner-only, second-factor-protected approved-hours CSV. More than eight hours in a shift or forty approved hours in its local starting week requires an explicit review reason. These are conservative review flags, not legal overtime or wage calculations, and no payroll transmission occurs. STAFF-001 inherits these location-scoped clock approval, denial and immediate revocation guarantees for wider roles; STAFF-002 inherits pay calculation on approved hours and corrections, and STAFF-004 retains Gusto and payroll handoff. Original events, proposals, approvals and replay identities survive native restore.

#### STAFF-004 — US payroll handoff

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** Owners review instructor pay, approved hours, sales commissions and tips (STAFF-005) by pay period in one report, classify W-2 and 1099 workers and export payroll-ready CSV or deliver to Gusto/ADP with reconciliation and error handling. Payroll providers execute pay and tax filings; see [Envelope](https://github.com/just-every/run-envelope) for payroll.

**Acceptance.**
1. Approve one pay period, export W-2 wages, 1099 teaching compensation, commissions and tips, import into a Gusto test workflow and reconcile worker IDs, hours, rates and totals without initiating unapproved payroll.

#### STAFF-005 — Sales commissions and tips

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** Owners set commission rules as a percentage or flat amount by item, product type (intro offers, memberships, packs, retail) or staff member, with effective dates. Each sale is attributed to the selling staff member at POS or checkout, including a membership sold at the desk or on a staff device (BILL-014), refunds and returns reverse commission proportionally in the period they occur, and tips taken at POS, checkout or appointment payment are attributed to the serving staff member. Staff see their own commission and tip breakdown, and the period pay report combines class and appointment pay, hourly pay, commissions and tips.

**Acceptance.**
1. Attribute a membership sale and a retail sale to one staff member under an item-level and a staff-level commission rate, refund part of the retail sale and take a tip on a private session at POS; compare the pay-period report and the Gusto export line by line with the expected commissions, reversal and tip.

#### STAFF-006 — Staff doors for every member action

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-AGENT-005, BASE-AGENT-007, BASE-AGENT-008 · *Rules:* D-2

**Requirement.** Every action a member can take for themselves in Strap has a staff door that does it on that member's behalf, whether the member is at the desk, on the phone or in an inbox conversation (MSG-001), from the member profile, the class roster or a staff phone or tablet (APP-004). Each staff door is gated by the STAFF-001 right named in the table below and keeps every fence, preview, policy, notice and once-only identity of the member door. It never shows staff raw card or account numbers, and it records a staff-made SEC-002 entry naming the acting staff member, role, right used, member, channel, before and after effect and any required reason. Where the member's own act is required (a signature, a recurring-payment authorization or ACH mandate, entering card or bank details, or confirming an SMS marketing opt-in), the staff door starts that act on the studio device or sends the member a secure link and never performs it for them. A member action added to this contract is complete only with its staff door row. Three member actions have no staff door because the incumbent front desk offers none: connecting the member's own Google Calendar (INT-002) and allowing push notifications on their own device (MSG-007), both member-held authorizations (staff change channel preferences under MEM-006 instead), and posting a public review (LEAD-008), which must be the member's own. Agent staff-door calls carry the classes of their effects: commitment for a binding contract change, money for a refund, debt waiver or collection, and standard for a reversible internal change. The company policy decides every authorized call. the current member-role studio commands are booking.book, booking.cancel, booking.spot, booking.offer.accept, attendance.self, checkin.code, waiver.acknowledge and notice.dismiss. Their role tags and staff-door mappings come from the real dispatch registry; a member-tagged command cannot ship without a mapping and a public-door coverage journey. Until STAFF-001, the native owner acts for front desk with all named rights; STAFF-001 inherits every table row’s permitted/denied-right and revocation acceptance case by name. Existing explicit staff privileges remain: desk-held capacity and reasoned booking overrides, and assigned staff roster check-in without the self-check-in window. Every current staff command keeps one canonical bookingAction or staffAction entry with actor, role, named right, member, channel, before/after effect and reason; SCHED-014 entries are reused rather than duplicated. A matching request identity reuses its effect and entry; changed inputs refuse. The code door reads the current code or issues its first one; a replacement needs an explicit reason and audit and never happens on read. Raw code bytes stay outside the audit. Staff launch an invitation or sign-in link for the current waiver; only the member’s own session acknowledges its exact version. A launch is a request, never a delivered-mail or acknowledged-waiver claim. One installation-local dispatch claim precedes native authentication; uncertain/interrupted dispatch is never automatically resent, and native restore carries launch intent only, with no dispatch authority. A staff waiver launch on a restored installation refuses with member_email_reconnect_required, with no launch, send or audit, until the owner reviews and reconnects member email in Studio; fresh installations allow the explicit launch, and the member’s own ordinary native sign-in stays unchanged. Staff may mark only the named member’s notice read without changing its content; the member retains read history labelled “Marked read by staff.” Current staff forms work at 1440 and 390 and record desk, phone or inbox channels; dedicated inbox and native-device doors inherit before shipping. Every future member capability and table row inherits the same staff door, named right, policy/preview/notice/once-only fences, member-owned authorization step and canonical audit before it ships: APP-001, APP-002, APP-003, APP-004, ATT-002, BILL-002, BILL-003, BILL-004, BILL-007, BILL-009, BILL-013, BILL-014, BILL-015, CHANNEL-003, CHANNEL-004, LEAD-005, LEAD-007, MEM-001, MEM-002, MEM-004, MEM-005, MEM-006, MSG-001, PLAN-001, PLAN-004, PLAN-005, PLAN-006, PLAN-007, PLAN-008, PLAN-009, PLAN-011, PLAN-012, SALE-001, SALE-002, SCHED-002, SCHED-005, SCHED-009, SCHED-012, SCHED-013, SEC-002, SEC-003, STAFF-001, SUCCESS-002, SUCCESS-003. Separate signing-launch scope is governed by STAFF-007; role grants remain STAFF-001.

| Member action | Member door | Staff door | STAFF-001 right | SEC-002 staff-made record |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Create an account and sign up | APP-001, APP-002 | MEM-001 | member-record | profile created, invitation sent |
| Update profile, contact details and emergency contact | APP-003 (`profile.save`), MEM-001 | MEM-001 (`profile.save.staff`; the owner until STAFF-001) | member-record | fields before and after |
| Choose private visit recognition | LEAD-006 (`recognition.preference.save`) | LEAD-006 (`recognition.launch`; member acts after native sign-in) | member-record | launch only, actor/member/channel/before/after; member-owned consent event |
| Choose booking/class email opt-out | MSG-002 (`mail.preference.save`) | MSG-002 (`mail.preference.launch`; member acts after native sign-in) | member-record | launch only; member-session choice records before/after and source |
| Change contact channels, marketing consent and preferences | MEM-006 | MEM-006 | member-record | preference before and after, consent source |
| Manage household members, guardians and payer | MEM-002, APP-003 | MEM-002 (payer authorizes own method, BILL-004) | member-record | household change, payer authorization |
| Complete an intake form | MEM-004 | MEM-004 | member-record; sensitive-notes for restricted answers | answers entered, provenance |
| Sign a waiver, policy or membership agreement, or acknowledge a waiver version | MEM-005, WAIVER-001 | STAFF-006 (`waiver.launch` for today’s version receipt), STAFF-007 (scoped signing launch), MEM-005 (signed documents/device; the member acts) | check-in | launch request, exact version and member’s own completion |
| Book a class or appointment | BOOK-001, SCHED-015, APP-001–003 | SCHED-010 | booking; override past a full roster or window | booking, credit used, override reason |
| Book a recurring class or appointment reservation | SCHED-005 | SCHED-013 | booking | series and per-occurrence outcomes |
| Choose or change a spot or reformer | SCHED-007 | SCHED-007 | booking | spot before and after |
| Join or leave a waitlist, or accept a waitlist offer | SCHED-011, MSG-004, APP-003 | SCHED-014 | booking; override for a chosen position | placement, position, removal or acceptance |
| Book a named guest | SCHED-012 | SCHED-012 | booking | guest booking, guest pass or charge |
| Cancel a booking before or after the cutoff | BOOK-002, SCHED-006, APP-003 | SCHED-014 | booking; fee-waiver for an early cancel after the cutoff | cancellation, early or late, fee, reason, notice state |
| Move or reschedule a booking | SCHED-015, APP-003 | SCHED-014 | booking; override into a full session | from and to sessions, credit carried |
| Restore a booking canceled in error | BOOK-001 (member books again) | SCHED-014 | booking; override into a full session | restore, fee reversed |
| Cancel one or all future occurrences of a series | SCHED-005 | SCHED-013 | booking | occurrences canceled, sessions returned |
| Report an absence and book a makeup | SCHED-009 | SCHED-009 | booking | absence, makeup booking |
| Enroll in or withdraw from a course or workshop | PLAN-009 | PLAN-009 | sale to enroll; contract-change to withdraw | enrollment, withdrawal effects |
| Join a livestream class | CHANNEL-003 | CHANNEL-003, SCHED-010 | booking | booking, join link resent |
| Accept an agent-offered class or an empty-place offer | SUCCESS-002, SUCCESS-003, LEAD-007 | SCHED-010 | booking | booking, offer accepted |
| Check in | ATT-001 | ATT-001 | check-in | check-in, alerts shown |
| Read or first-issue a check-in code or key tag | ATT-001 (`checkin.code`) | STAFF-006 (`checkin.code.staff`); replacement only explicitly with a reason | check-in | issuance/read revision; reasoned rotation, never raw code bytes |
| Mark an in-app notice read | SCHED-008, MSG-004 (`notice.dismiss`) | STAFF-006 (`notice.dismiss.staff`) | member-record | before/after read state, actor and channel; member-visible staff-read history |
| Buy a drop-in, class pack or pass | PLAN-001, BILL-002, APP-001 | SCHED-010, SALE-002 | sale | sale, tender, seller |
| Buy an intro offer | PLAN-004 | PLAN-004, SALE-002 | sale; price for an eligibility exception | sale, eligibility exception reason |
| Buy an auto-renewing membership or accept an intro conversion or win-back offer | PLAN-001, PLAN-004, LEAD-005, BILL-002 | BILL-014 | membership-sale | sale, authorization, signed agreement |
| Buy on-demand video access | CHANNEL-004 | SALE-002 | sale | sale, access granted |
| Buy a gift card | SALE-001 | SALE-001, SALE-002 | sale | sale, gift card issued |
| Buy a retail product | SALE-002 (sold in person) | SALE-002 | sale | sale, stock movement |
| Redeem a gift card | SALE-001 | SALE-001, SALE-002 | sale | redemption, remaining liability |
| Redeem a discount code | SALE-001 | SALE-002 | sale; price for a manual adjustment | code use, adjustment reason |
| Apply account credit | BILL-015 | BILL-015, SALE-002 | sale to apply; refund to issue or remove | credit applied, balance after |
| Pay with a saved card or bank method | BILL-002, BILL-004 | SALE-002 (card on file under recorded consent) | sale | card-on-file charge, consent reference |
| Get an invoice or receipt | BILL-009, APP-003 | BILL-009 | sale | document sent, recipient |
| Pay an outstanding invoice or retry a failed charge | BILL-007, APP-003 | BILL-007, BILL-013 | collection | attempt and outcome |
| Pay a late-cancel or no-show fee | ATT-002 | ATT-002 | collection to collect; fee-waiver to waive | fee collected or waived, reason |
| Add, replace or verify a card or bank method | BILL-003, BILL-004 | BILL-004 (member taps or enters on the studio device) | collection | method saved, masked details |
| Remove a payment method or change the default | BILL-004 | BILL-004 | collection | method removed, default before and after |
| Change plan | PLAN-005 | PLAN-005 | contract-change | plan change, confirmed preview |
| Freeze a membership | PLAN-006 | PLAN-011 | contract-change | freeze type, dates, fee |
| Cancel a membership or its auto-renewal, including from a renewal notice | PLAN-007, PLAN-008 | PLAN-012 | contract-change | termination, reason code, comment |
| Refer a friend | LEAD-005 | LEAD-005 | member-record | referral attributed |
| Request access to or erasure of their data | SEC-003 | SEC-003 | privacy (owners) | request, identity check, outcome |

**Acceptance.**
1. For every row of the table, as a front-desk user granted that row's right, perform the action for a member through the 390-pixel staff view and the staff HTTP command: the persisted outcome, preview and member notice equal the member door's, and exactly one staff-made SEC-002 record names the staff member, right, member and channel.
2. Repeat as a front-desk user without that right (refused, no writes) and with a preparation-only agent key (refused, or held as a draft where the row's clause allows one).
3. Rows that start the member's own act complete only after the member's step on the studio device or link.
4. A coverage test enumerates every member-role command in the studio HTTP interface and fails when one has no staff command for the same action.

#### RPT-001 — Revenue collections and member trends

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** Dashboards report active members, new memberships, cancellations, gross/net sales, collections, refunds, fees and overdue balances by plan, location and period, with explicit metric definitions and drill-through to source records.

**Acceptance.**
1. Run a month of purchases, cancellations, returned ACH and refunds, filter the dashboard and drill each total to its ledger; independent sums of those records match the report.
2. Semantics for studio (RPT-001): the native owner acts for finance and managers until STAFF-001, which inherits report grants, denial and immediate revocation.
3. The current projection covers every current pack, period/unlimited plan, desk sale, plan-change charge, external receipt/refund, assessed attendance or late-move fee and reversal, and retained booking cancellation.
4. Future PLAN-001/004/006/007/008/009/011, BILL-001–015, SALE-001–004, STUDIO-002 and STAFF-001 inherit source-linked reporting for each new membership, product, collection/provider state, cancellation, tax, fee, refund, location and permission before shipping; every later clause adding a financial or membership record inherits the same obligation.
5. Report dates use inclusive studio-local calendar bounds on each ledger record's createdAt (cancel actions use at); calculations use signed exact USD cents.
6. Gross sales are positive non-fee charges, sales credits are negative non-fee charges, net sales is their signed sum, and fees are signed attendance/late-move assessments and reversals.
7. External collections are positive payment records, refunds are the magnitude of negative payments, and net collections is their signed sum; balance credit never claims cash refunded. Named plan/type filters attribute only through actual pack, charge or booking links; ordinary receipts remain Unallocated. New plans count created packs, including accepted renewals, never drafts. Active members is explicitly a current snapshot of active member identities with an unexpired pack and remaining credits or a visits/unlimited entitlement; it is independent of the event window. Booking cancellations count retained action identities, including restored cancellations, separately from unavailable membership churn. Current outstanding balances sum positive all-time member balances and member credit sums negative balances' magnitudes, each linked to all its signed ledger sources; both are unavailable under a plan filter because receipts may be unallocated. No due dates, historical member-status events, membership termination, ACH returns or processor costs exist, so overdue, historical active trends, membership churn, ACH returns and processor fees are labelled unavailable. The location filter uses the single studio's current label until STUDIO-002 supplies historical location attribution. Every event total exposes its exact retained sources, including unchanged signed amounts, references and linked identity; reads never create financial effects.

#### RPT-002 — Occupancy demand and attendance

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001

**Requirement.** Owners compare booked, waitlisted, attended, late-canceled and no-show counts, unique visitors and usable seat occupancy by class time, instructor, room, service and location; canceled sessions and aggregator inventory are identified in denominators. Cancellation counts come from the SCHED-014 cancellations list and separate member from staff cancellations and early from late. the owner opens Attendance reports or the owner-only attendance-report JSON door. Filters select classes by inclusive studio-local start-date range, exact local start time, instructor, room, service and the current location; room, service and location labels ignore case and repeated whitespace. Booked counts current confirmed bookings, including attended and no-shows; waitlisted includes unclaimed offers. Attendance reflects corrections and unique visitors counts distinct attended people across all selected classes. Cancellation counts use retained action identities once even after restoration, separately by early/late type and member/staff/system actor; a self-cancel by an instructor on their own booking is a member cancellation. Each class links to its roster, booking identities and cancellation actions. Occupancy is the sum of currently occupied seats (including held offers) divided by current usable seats, rounded to two decimal percent; usable seats are the smaller of configured capacity and currently enabled room spots, with online and desk-held pools capped together. Canceled sessions remain visible but contribute zero seats; no usable seats means occupancy is unavailable. The report identifies the absence of connected aggregator inventory rather than inventing it. STUDIO-002 inherits location-scoped filters, CHANNEL-001/CHANNEL-002 inherit separately identified aggregator inventory in the same denominator, SCHED-002 inherits appointments, and SCHED-005/009/012/013 inherit their record kinds before shipping.

**Acceptance.**
1. Fill and partly attend classes with disabled reformers, a waitlist and a cancellation, then inspect each denominator and drill to rosters and source bookings.
2. Member and staff, early and late cancellations appear as separate counts and drill to the cancellations list.
3. SCHED-014 obligation before this capability ships: SCHED-014 historical late cancellations counted once from retained action identities; assert the inherited behavior through its public doors.

#### RPT-003 — Retention and renewal cohorts

*Tier:* Core · *From:* RPT-003 · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-006

**Requirement.** Retention reports show member and revenue churn, renewal rates, tenure, attendance frequency, freeze and reactivation, churn by studio-defined termination reason code (PLAN-012) and freeze type (PLAN-011) and cohorts by acquisition month and offer. Imported history is labeled and included with declared definition differences.

**Acceptance.**
1. Import an 18-month member history, renew and cancel plans after cutover and inspect continuous cohorts; drill churn and recovered renewals to original and new contract and payment evidence; terminate two members under PLAN-012 with reason codes "Moved away" and "Price" and see churn by reason show each once.
2. RPT-003 current the owner reports retained native credit, visit-limited and unlimited plans together with explicitly reviewed historical terms.
3. Acquisition uses the earliest retained term's studio-local month and original offer, ordered by creation instant then ID; later native offers stay in that cohort.
4. The documented `strap-retention-history-v1` import maps one member's non-overlapping historical terms to their first native cutover pack, retains contract and payment references, reported visits, source definition differences, fingerprint and reviewer, refuses changed previews and conflicting repeat imports, and changes no access or balances.
5. Imported source renewals link the next historical term or the reviewed cutover pack; native completed renewals require an applied PLAN-005 change or non-void recurring invoice and a distinct linked successor.
6. Renewal rate divides renewed completed terms by completed terms, with unavailable zero denominators.
7. Period churn uses an explicit UTC interval (from, to], defaulting to the last 30 complete UTC days.

#### RPT-008 — Retention measures and source reconciliation

*Tier:* Core · *From:* RPT-003 · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-006

**Requirement.** Retention measures retain their original source and current native agreement, cancellation, charge and payment evidence across the owner report and downloads.

**Acceptance.**
1. Opening members have accepted native recurring agreements at start that have not ended then. A member is lost only when all opening agreements end in the interval, no accepted agreement remains at end and no retained native plan provides access at end. Gross canceled recurring revenue churn divides lost opening monthly commitment by opening monthly commitment, using the initial acknowledged price or latest non-void invoice due by start, divided by its accepted month interval. Rates round to two decimals; zero or unknown denominators are unavailable. Native termination, freeze and resumption counts retain applied reason codes and confirmed started types; scheduled, withdrawn and superseded events are not completed events. Historical source reason codes remain labeled, and absent historical churn denominators and freeze events are explicitly declared unavailable rather than inferred. Tenure spans earliest term to the earlier of the report instant and latest retained expiry, in whole elapsed days; frequency pools reported historical visits with corrected native attended bookings from the reviewed cutover onward when history is imported, using visits times 30 divided by tenure days, unavailable at zero tenure. Native gross collected renewal cents require explicit allocations to the linked positive successor charge; imported collections remain labeled source observations with original references. Source, agreement, cancellation, charge and payment evidence link from the same filtered owner screen, HTTP, CSV and XLSX at 1440 and 390. These are committed recurring-revenue and gross-collection measures, not cash or net revenue after refunds. RPT-004 inherits cash and liability definitions; RPT-006 retains scheduled delivery; MOVE-001 through MOVE-006 retain incumbent migrations and MOVE-009 retains broader historical ingestion and erasure.

#### RPT-004 — Cash earned revenue and liabilities

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-DATA-006

**Requirement.** Finance separates cash collected, invoiced dues, earned revenue, unused-pack and gift-card liabilities, deferred membership revenue and tax; recognition policy and adjustments are explicit and tie to the operational ledger.

**Acceptance.**
1. Sell an annual plan, ten-pack and gift card, consume some value and refund part; monthly cash/earned/deferred totals reconcile to invoices, entitlements and recognized service periods.
2. the MFA-protected human owner reads monthly recorded net cash, signed invoiced service dues, earned and deferred service value, unused-pack value as a subset of deferred revenue, and signed gift-card liabilities.
3. Original accepted time-based intervals and completed finite-pack services use cumulative half-up USD-cent calculations.
4. Gift funding is a liability, redemption is noncash tender and recorded funding refunds reduce cash and liability once.
5. Signed receivables and unclassified value remain separate; a nonzero reconciliation difference is visible.
6. Historical attendance corrections restate retained-record projections, not a closed accounting journal.
7. Source-linked CSV and XLSX preserve the same monthly projections and ledger facts.
8. Original accepted time-based service prices are recognized over their original accepted service intervals using elapsed time and cumulative half-up rounding to USD cents; each monthly earned amount is the difference between cumulative endpoints. Finite-pack service value is recognized only after an attended service has ended, using the original accepted price and original credit quantity. Reservations, check-in before service completion, no-shows, forfeiture, expiry and manual credit reductions do not themselves earn finite-pack revenue. Expired unused value remains visible without automatic breakage recognition. Attendance corrections preserve original history and may restate retained-record projections. Changed or frozen plans, cancellation credits, fees and unsupported adjustments retain their signed amounts and source references as unclassified value until supported classification exists; they are never silently omitted or treated as earned. Cash means recorded receipts less recorded refunds, not verified processor settlement. Invoiced dues, signed receivables, earned value, deferred value, the unused-pack subset and gift liability remain distinct and reconcile without counting the unused-pack subset twice. Gift funding creates liability; redemption is noncash tender against a separate sale; funding refunds and returned gift tender retain their distinct once-only effects. Tax remains unavailable, not zero or assumed excluded, pending BILL-012 classification and reversal evidence. The human owner reporting door requires SEC-004 MFA; SEC-004 separately governs its explicit finance-report grant, MFA and revocation, and this ruling neither removes that grant nor supplies STAFF-001 or SEC-005 role breadth. This is provisional studio management reporting, not a closed accounting journal, accounting posting, GAAP certification, tax filing or accounting or tax advice. PACK-001, PLAN-001 and PLAN-002 retain original sale and entitlement duties; ATT-001 and ATT-003 retain attendance and correction provenance; MEM-005 retains applicable service-policy and signature duties; PLAN-006, PLAN-007, PLAN-011, PLAN-012 and BILL-008 retain freeze, termination and refund duties. AUDIT-001 and SEC-002, ARCHIVE-001 and MOVE-011, RPT-006 and SEC-003 retain their audit, portable records, guarded restore, export and retention duties. This policy approval proves no missing implementation, provider result, legal conclusion or original acceptance assertion. Supported finite-pack freeze classification, 27 October 2026: an access-only freeze and expiry extension preserve the original accepted price and original credit quantity; only completed attended service earns value. The elapsed-time journey proves the unchanged earned and deferred cents and retained unused value after extended expiry. Frozen time-based plans, changed consideration, service-refund recognition and fees remain explicit unclassified gaps. Supported ordinary finite-pack refund classification, 27 October 2026: when a retained known ordinary refund does not exceed original unearned value and the pack has no changed consideration, initiation fee or mixed charges, the refund reduces deferred and unused-pack value and establishes its separate payable. Completed attended service remains earned at the original price and quantity; recorded settlement changes cash and payable only. Original credit movements and service periods remain intact. Cooling-off allocations, mixed fees, changed consideration, refunds exceeding unearned value and unknown valuations remain explicitly unclassified or unavailable. Supported ordinary unlimited time-based refund classification, 27 October 2026: where one unchanged original unlimited service charge has a confirmed ordinary termination and known refund within its unearned value, recognition stops at that effective termination. The report and both downloads retain the original accepted service start and end and separately expose serviceStoppedAt; the original interval is not rewritten or shortened. A known approved refund reduces deferred value and establishes its separate payable; partial external settlement reduces recorded cash and that payable once. The unused-pack subset stays zero for time-based service. Partial funding never invents a receipt or removes unpaid original dues, and unused deferred value does not become earned after termination. Periodic visit-limited terms, frozen time-based terms, changed consideration, cooling-off, mixed fees and unsupported valuations remain unclassified or unavailable. The earlier approvals with riders for access-only finite-pack freezes and ordinary finite-pack refunds remain binding. For one unchanged original unlimited-service charge with a confirmed applied ordinary termination and a retained known approved refund within original unearned value, cumulative recognition stops at the confirmed effective termination using the original accepted price and full original accepted service interval, with half-up USD-cent rounding. Original accepted serviceStart and serviceEnd remain unchanged in the report and both downloads; serviceStoppedAt records the separate termination. The refund reduces only original unearned deferred value and establishes a separate payable. The time-based unused-pack subset remains zero. Partial receipt funding neither creates a receipt nor removes unpaid original invoiced dues. Recording or replaying external settlement changes cash and payable once, without changing earned value, deferred value, original entitlement terms or original invoice debt. This recognition policy does not replace the native approved refund valuation, cancellation authority, final-bill policy or entitlement effects. Periodic visit-limited cancellation refunds, frozen time-based terms, changed consideration, cooling-off, initiation fees, mixed consideration, excess refunds and unsupported valuations remain unclassified or unavailable until supported classification and public-door assertions exist. These exclusions preserve the full target and do not complete it. PLAN-006, PLAN-007, PLAN-011, PLAN-012 and BILL-008 retain their original policy, authority, entitlement and refund duties. SEC-004 retains MFA and its existing explicit finance-report grant. Recorded cash is not verified processor settlement, and no production tax, filing, legal or accounting certification is claimed.

#### RPT-005 — Lead intro and campaign conversion

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001

**Requirement.** Staff see source-to-lead, intro purchase, first visit, membership conversion and repeat-attendance funnels with campaign cost and attributed revenue; unknown source and incomplete follow-up remain visible.

**Acceptance.**
1. Create leads from two channels, run an intro campaign, convert one and lapse another; funnel stages, spending and attributed revenue drill to the recorded events without inventing a conversion.
2. the native owner records an optional immutable source when creating an inquiry.
3. Each member belongs to their first retained inquiry (creation instant, then ID), with absent sources explicitly unknown and later inquiries linked without counting acquisitions or visits twice.
4. The exact trimmed source filter selects the same member rows on screen and HTTP.
5. Retained intro-booking and external membership stage-entry events remain separate from purchases and native conversion.
6. Current corrected confirmed attended bookings count only when their class start is from the first inquiry through the report instant, inclusive; two visits mean repeat attendance.
7. Each row links its retained inquiry events and authoritative rosters.
8. Incomplete follow-up is a current inquiry outside membership/lost, overdue when its due instant is before the report instant; lost is recorded, never inferred. Intro purchases, native conversion, campaigns, cost and revenue attribution remain unavailable. Full acceptance remains required.

#### RPT-006 — Report exports and scheduled delivery

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-DATA-006, BASE-MAIL-001

**Requirement.** Permissioned users download filtered transaction CSV and XLSX including payment references, fees, refunds, tax and payouts, export the upcoming autopay schedule (BILL-013), upcoming plan endings (RPT-007) and the cancellations list (SCHED-014), and schedule revenue, attendance, retention and pay reports to authorized recipients with the same filters and totals.

**Acceptance.**
1. Download and open a month's CSV/XLSX in a spreadsheet, compare row totals with the dashboard and receive its scheduled email; remove a recipient's rights and confirm the next delivery is blocked.
2. SCHED-014 obligation before this capability ships: SCHED-014 cancellations with identical screen/CSV filters in its XLSX and scheduled exports; assert the inherited behavior through its public doors.
3. Current download owner-only downloads use the existing revenue, attendance and cancellation projections and the same screen filters; no export grants broader access.
4. CSV headers are plain strings and every data cell is a JSON value, matching the existing cancellation and portable-exit CSV convention.
5. XLSX Sources cells contain literal text, numbers or booleans, never formulas; Totals contains every dashboard metric, and Context records filters, the studio time zone and metric definitions.
6. Amounts are exact USD cents; unavailable values remain JSON null or blank XLSX cells, distinct from zero.
7. Revenue and attendance date bounds remain inclusive studio-local dates.
8. Cancellation XLSX preserves the same retained identities and date, class, actor and type filters as the cancellation screen and CSV. These download capabilities do not complete the scheduled-delivery or other report-kind requirements. Studio-report downloads: retention and renewal XLSX preserve the existing CSV columns and filters; retention adds the same cohort metrics and unavailable fields as its screen/HTTP report. Teaching-pay CSV/XLSX use the existing native-owner export authority and earned-class projection, with inclusive exact-instant start bounds and an optional instructor filter; screen links export the unfiltered owner summary. XLSX keeps nested source identities, rule/calculation and adjustment records as literal JSON text, USD amounts as numeric cents, and unavailable values blank. Its pay Totals sheet preserves the configured subtotal separately from an unavailable overall total. Each workbook records its report instant, effective filters, studio time zone and definitions. These downloads neither run payroll nor send messages; the full scheduled-delivery and autopay acceptance remains required.

#### RPT-007 — Upcoming plan endings and renewal worklist

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001 · *Rules:* D-3

**Requirement.** Staff see a forward list of fixed-term memberships reaching their end date, auto-renewals scheduled to cancel, staff-scheduled terminations with their reason code (PLAN-012), freezes ending, intro offers expiring and packs expiring or down to a configured number of credits within a chosen window such as the next 30 days, with member, plan, end date, remaining value, last visit, renewal status and contact permission. From the list staff create assigned renewal follow-up tasks in the lead pipeline (LEAD-002) or enroll members in an approved renewal sequence (AUTO-001); a member who renews leaves the list and one already in follow-up is marked. The list exports through RPT-006. The governing outcome is recorded in [RULINGS.md RL-106](RULINGS.md#rl-106-upcoming-plan-endings-and-renewal-worklist).

**Acceptance.**
1. Give members a fixed-term plan ending in 20 days, another ending in 40 days, an auto-renewal set to cancel in 10 days, an intro expiring next week and a pack with one credit left; the 30-day list shows exactly the four in range with correct dates and remaining value, creating a follow-up task assigns it once, renewing one member removes them from the list, and the CSV export matches the screen; a PLAN-012 termination scheduled in 15 days then appears with its reason code, and canceling it removes it.
2. Current RPT-007 native one row per unexpired, unrenewed pack combines in-range end events and freeze resumptions with low-credit eligibility.
3. Visit/unlimited plans have fixed native access terms; an active recurring invoice agreement alone is not an ending.
4. Confirmed cancellation uses its effective date and owner-entered reason code (default other); withdrawal restores the retained pack expiry.
5. A freeze-only row uses its resumption date.
6. Value is receipt-allocated unused prepaid elapsed access at report time, excluding confirmed freeze time, rounded half-up under the existing cancellation valuation; unallocated eligible receipts remain unavailable, and zero recorded prepaid value is zero, never a claim of payment.
7. This is no refund quote.
8. Channel permission is shown independently for email, SMS and in-app marketing; no permission is inferred from booking email. The owner previews and approves native renewal sequences, then enrolls each eligible pack once. Explicit queue review rechecks permission, source, pause and SMS quiet hours, recording at most one native in-app message; SMS/email remain held without a provider transport. Source-checked follow-up is one private assigned LEAD-002 task per pack, with replay and changed-source fences. AUTO-001 inherits automated triggers, budgets and provider execution; MSG-008/MEM-006 carry channel permission, suppression and quiet hours. The current conservative defaults await ct approval. Source-bound native renewal enrollment and private once-only follow-up tasks use the exact reviewed sequence authority, MEM-006 channel consent and AUTO-001. Renewal, opt-out, pause and changed source stop an ineligible queued effect. A previously reviewed bounded sequence may authorize its stated messages without a new approval at each step. Provider-autopay endings, external delivery and scoped staff work remain required; an agent call carries its server-computed classes under company policy.

### Named integrations and developer access

#### INT-001 — QuickBooks Online accounting sync

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** The owner maps studio products, locations, tax, tender and liability accounts to QuickBooks Online, directly or through an explicit connector; invoices, payments, refunds, fees and payouts sync once, with exceptions and reconciliation. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection.

**Acceptance.**
1. Connect a QBO sandbox, map a pack and retail SKU, sync a month and rerun it; no duplicates appear, AR/liabilities/payout totals agree and a missing mapping enters an actionable queue.

#### INT-002 — Google Calendar schedule publication

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** Members and instructors publish their own booked or assigned classes and appointments to Google Calendar with local time, updates and cancellations. One-way sync is labeled; calendar edits cannot silently change studio bookings. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection.

**Acceptance.**
1. Connect Google Calendar, book and reschedule a class, cancel it and verify calendar changes and timezone; editing the external event leaves the authoritative booking unchanged.

#### INT-003 — Kisi membership-linked access

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** Approved membership and location eligibility grants Kisi door access; cancellation, freeze, nonpayment suspension and revocation apply the studio's grace rules, with visible pending/failed device updates and human override audit. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection.

**Acceptance.**
1. Connect a controlled Kisi site, activate, freeze and cancel a plan, interrupt the connector and verify desired versus confirmed door state and an actionable exception rather than a false revoked indicator.

#### INT-004 — HybridAF membership-linked access

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** The studio maps memberships and locations to HybridAF access groups; grants, expiries, suspensions and manual overrides reconcile against membership state and retain provider acknowledgments. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection.

**Acceptance.**
1. Connect a controlled HybridAF site, grant access, expire a plan and replay the update; one correct grant/revocation results and an unavailable provider stays visibly unresolved.

#### INT-005 — Mailchimp consent-aware audiences

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-AGENT-005, BASE-AGENT-007, BASE-AGENT-008 · *Rules:* D-3

**Requirement.** Owners synchronize selected member segments, lifecycle tags and marketing consent with Mailchimp; campaign unsubscribes return to Strap and suppress queued marketing. Transactional booking notices are separately configured. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection.

**Acceptance.**
1. Sync intro and lapsed segments to a Mailchimp test audience, unsubscribe externally, resync and run a campaign; the unsubscribed member remains suppressed and is not silently re-added.
2. LEAD-008 obligation before feedback or marketing synchronization ships: feedback requests are marketing and marketing opt-outs suppress queued requests immediately; individual ratings and private comments never go to Mailchimp, Google or another marketing tool.

#### INT-006 — Meta lead ads and conversion tracking

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** Studios connect Meta/Facebook Ads to ingest consented lead-form submissions and report approved lead, intro and membership conversions with deduplication and attribution. Sensitive intake or health notes never enter ad payloads. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection.

**Acceptance.**
1. Submit a Meta test lead, purchase an intro and membership, replay callbacks and inspect one deduplicated conversion each; turn off ad consent and verify no disallowed tracking payload.

#### INT-007 — Google Analytics booking attribution

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** The website/widget reports consented schedule, checkout and purchase events to Google Analytics 4 with campaign attribution and unique transaction IDs, excluding member contact and restricted intake data. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection.

**Acceptance.**
1. With and without analytics consent, complete widget checkout and inspect GA4 DebugView; consented events appear once with matching transaction value and no identifying payload fields.

#### INT-008 — Zapier and signed outbound events

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** Owners connect Zapier triggers/actions and signed webhooks for leads, bookings, attendance, membership and payment changes with scoped authority, event IDs, delivery history and safe replay; external automation cannot approve financial exceptions. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection. API-002 obligation before this capability ships: deliver only events that an integration key's scopes permit and its owner subscribed, including `attendance.changed` only for `attendance:read`, under the same owner-only lifecycle, rotation, revocation and restore fences, and replace the not-delivered label only with the honest outcome of an actual delivery attempt. API-003 obligation before this capability ships: `attendance.changed` carries only the API-003 outcome fields (never reasons, notes, money or contact details) and goes only to keys granted `attendance:read` that subscribed to it. Transport and record details are in [Studio mechanisms](docs/contract/MECHANISMS.md#int-008).

**Acceptance.**
1. Trigger a booking and lead in a Zapier test connection, receive signed events, replay them and submit an allowed action; inspect idempotence and refusal of refund approval.

#### INT-009 — Google Business Profile booking

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001

**Requirement.** The studio's Google Business Profile carries a Book action linking to its branded schedule and intro checkout (APP-002), with Google-source attribution in lead and conversion reports. When Reserve with Google accepts Strap as the studio's booking provider, classes are bookable in Google Search and Maps under the same online-capacity fence. MOVE-011 obligation before this capability ships: its studio records and documents must join the portable exit with stable relationships and provenance; a native restore keeps its studio billing and delivery disconnected until the human owner reviews and approves reconnection.

**Acceptance.**
1. Add the booking link to a test Google Business Profile, follow it signed out, buy an intro offer and see the lead and conversion attributed to Google; with a Reserve with Google feed enabled, book the last online seat from Google and from the app concurrently and verify one booking.

#### API-001 — Authenticated studio HTTP interface

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-INPUT-001

**Requirement.** Documented JSON state and command endpoints let native human sessions operate their permitted studio flows, while revocable bearer preparation keys use a separate context and command door and cannot cross into human approval.

**Acceptance.**
1. Read authenticated state, perform a member booking, prepare a pack with a bearer key, refuse its approval and revoke it through the owner interface.

#### API-002 — Scoped integration lifecycle

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-DATA-006, BASE-AGENT-005, BASE-AGENT-007, BASE-AGENT-008, BASE-INPUT-001 · *Rules:* D-2

**Requirement.** Owners create least-privilege, location-aware application credentials, rotate and revoke them, set permitted event subscriptions and inspect usage and failures. Pagination, schema versions and documented rate responses make integrations operable without exposing full owner data. this clause's target is exactly the catalogue below and the lifecycle around it; further read scopes (such as contact details or money) and any write scope each need their own owner-approved clause and acceptance before they join the catalogue. Native secret issuance uses the owner's app session. An authorized agent integration-lifecycle call carries access and security under company policy; a preparation-only credential lacks that scope. Secrets in the native-person flow are issued in the app: the Integrations page form creates a key and rotates its secret (`integration.create`, `integration.rotate`), the JSON command door refuses both even for the owner (403 `integration_secret_in_app_only`), and the owner revokes a key or changes its events on the page or through the owner command door (`integration.revoke`, `integration.events`); preparation-only agent keys, integration keys, members, instructors and kiosks are refused with no writes. A key's secret is 256 random bits shown once; Strap stores only its SHA-256 hash, as for preparation-only agent keys, and a key works only while the owner who created it keeps the same active owner access. The fixed read-only catalogue holds `roster:read` and the separately granted `attendance:read`. The `roster:read` scope returns classes (id, title, service, UTC start and end, capacity, published or cancelled) with each booking's id, status (booked, waitlisted, offered or cancelled) and the attendee's first name plus last initial; it never carries attendance, money, packs, payments, fees, balances, contact details, notes, cancellation reasons or instructor names. The separate GET resource the documented studio door requires `attendance:read`, which is off by default and granted explicitly by the owner only when creating a new key on Integrations. Scopes stay fixed for a key's life: no existing key gains attendance and rotation never widens scopes. For a key created before API-003 shipped it uses the roster's class paging and returns only each class id and each currently booked booking's `bookingId` and `attended` boolean. True means its current outcome is attended, by check-in or owner correction; false covers unrecorded, no-show and excused without distinguishing them. Waitlisted, offered and cancelled bookings are omitted. No names, outcome labels, times, actors, channels, kiosk ids, fees, credits, reasons, corrections or notes appear; a key also granted `roster:read` may join on bookingId. Naming no-show, excused or cancellation outcomes, or a member reference, is API-003, which applies only to keys created after it shipped. Every API-002 rule applies to both scopes, including the owner-only lifecycle, check order, shared per-key rate limit, logs, rotation, revocation, location, erasure and restore fences. Every key covers the studio's one location (the Studio settings location) until STUDIO-002 adds location scopes. The integration door (the documented studio door) answers GET only: any other method is refused 405 `read_only`, and any resource outside the key's scopes, including payments, balances, packs and people, 403 `scope_not_granted`, with no writes. Every request names `strap-schema-version: 1`; a missing or unknown version is refused 400 with the supported versions. Roster pages hold 1–100 classes (default 50) in UTC start-then-id order after an opaque cursor; an invalid limit or cursor is refused, never clamped. Each key may make 60 counted requests in any rolling 60 seconds: every request with a live secret counts, whatever its outcome, except those refused by the limit, which are answered 429 `rate_limited` with `Retry-After` in whole seconds (1–60) until the oldest counted request leaves the window. Strap checks the secret, then the limit, the method, the schema version and the scope. Rotation commits the new secret and the old secret's revocation together; revocation stops every secret of the key at once, and a revoked key cannot be rotated or resubscribed. The owner who created a key reads its usage and failures: the newest 100 requests per key with time, method, path, status and refusal code, never a query string, member data or secret; a request with an unknown secret cannot be attributed and is not logged. A key subscribes only to events its scopes permit (`roster:read`: `class.changed` and `booking.changed`; `attendance:read`: `attendance.changed`); Strap records the subscriptions and labels them not delivered until INT-008 delivers signed events. Keys, secrets, subscriptions, logs and rate counts never travel in native archives or the portable exit, so a restored studio has no integration keys and refuses every old secret (ARCHIVE-001, MOVE-011); erased people never appear in either scope. Named carriers inherit these rules with their own before-shipping acceptance: STUDIO-002 (location scopes), INT-008 (signed delivery of subscribed, scope-permitted events) and SEC-003 (used-member erasure leaves both scopes). Transport and record details are in [Studio mechanisms](docs/contract/MECHANISMS.md#api-002).

**Acceptance.**
1. Create a read-roster integration for one location, traverse paginated results, attempt financial data and writes, rotate/revoke the key and inspect failures and rate-limit behavior.
2. A roster-only key is refused attendance with 403 and its roster still has no attendance; a dual-scope key created before API-003 shipped reads true for check-in and owner-corrected attendance, false for no-show and excused, and no money or reason fields.
3. Erased people are absent and a native restored studio refuses the key; `docs/INTEGRATIONS.md` documents the separate scope.

#### API-003 — Attendance outcome sharing

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-DATA-006, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-INPUT-001

**Requirement.** Owners let another application, such as a front-desk display or a studio dashboard, read who booked, attended, missed or cancelled each class through a separate explicit `attendance:read` scope on a new integration key, with a stable per-key member reference and short names, and without reasons, notes, health answers, money or contact details. `attendance:read` is a separate explicit scope that the owner grants only when creating a new key on Integrations, where the form lists the exact shared fields before the key exists. Scopes stay fixed for a key's life: no existing key gains attendance, rotation never widens scopes, and `roster:read` stays attendance-free. Every API-002 rule applies unchanged: owner-only lifecycle with secrets shown only in the app and stored as SHA-256 hashes, GET only, `strap-schema-version: 1`, pages of 1–100 classes (default 50) in UTC start-then-id order, 60 counted requests per key in any rolling 60 seconds shared with every resource, the owner-only usage log, immediate rotation and revocation, exclusion from native archives and the portable exit, and the studio's one location until STUDIO-002 adds location scopes. GET the documented studio door returns, for each class on the page, its id, title, service, UTC start and end and status (published or cancelled), and for each seat with an outcome a row of exactly: `bookingId`, `memberRef`, `attendee`, `outcome`, `checkedInAt`, `checkInChannel`, `cancelledAt`, `cancelledBy`, `corrected`, `correctedFrom`, `correctedAt` and `source`. `outcome` is one of `booked` (a confirmed seat nobody has checked in or marked), `attended`, `no_show`, `excused`, `late_cancel` and `early_cancel`; the outcome is the booking's current state, and a late arrival is not a state, so a consumer compares `checkedInAt` with the class start. A waitlisted or offered place has no outcome and is omitted, as is a cancellation that was not a late or early cancel choice (a move, an expired offer, a waitlist release, or the studio cancelling the class, which the class status shows). `checkedInAt` and `checkInChannel` are set only for a real check-in (roster or kiosk) and are null when the owner corrected the seat to attended. `cancelledAt` and `cancelledBy` (`member`, `staff` or `system`) are set only on a cancelled seat. `corrected` is true when the owner corrected the outcome, `correctedFrom` is the outcome before the latest correction (`no_show` or `late_cancel`) and `correctedAt` its UTC time, never the reason. `source` is `native`, or `imported` for a row that MOVE-009 brings in. `attendee` is the first name plus last initial, as in the roster. `memberRef` is `mref_` plus the first 128 bits, in hex, of HMAC-SHA-256 over the person id under a random secret held only by that key: it is the same for one member in every class and across secret rotation, differs between members, and cannot be joined across two keys or to a person id. The resource never carries correction, cancellation or waiver reasons, notes, staff alerts, waiver state, intake or health answers, contact details, kiosk ids, check-in codes, full names, person ids, money, fees, credits, packs, balances, instructor names or pay, or an erased person. A key created with `attendance:read` under API-002 alone, before this clause shipped, keeps its boolean-only resource and gains no field. A key subscribed to `attendance.changed` is told it is recorded and labelled not delivered until INT-008 delivers signed events. Carriers inherit these rules with their own before-shipping acceptance: INT-008 (the event carries only these fields), SEC-003 (used and imported member erasure removes the rows and the reference), STUDIO-002 (location scopes), MOVE-009 (imported rows and their source) and LEAD-006, MEM-001 and MEM-006 (a member sharing opt-out, if one is added, removes that member's rows). The owner’s explicit grant on a new key remains required. MEM-006 now also lets a member remove their rows from existing integration keys; it never widens a key’s scope. Transport and record details are in [Studio mechanisms](docs/contract/MECHANISMS.md#api-003).

**Acceptance.**
1. A new key created on Integrations with `attendance:read` (the form lists the fields; a roster-only key is refused the resource with 403 and its roster, even after rotation or crafted scope fields, carries no attendance) reads classes and rows with the exact field allowlist; asserts booked, attended (check-in), no_show, excused (owner correction), late_cancel and early_cancel with `cancelledBy`, `corrected`, `correctedFrom` and `correctedAt` (a late cancel corrected to early reads early_cancel, corrected from late_cancel), no check-in time for a corrected attendance, waitlisted, offered and studio-cancelled seats omitted, `source` imported once a row is marked, `memberRef` equal for one member across classes and rotation and different between members and between two keys, full name, email, phone, ids of people, reasons, money and instructor absent, an erased person absent, paging, the shared rate limit and usage log, `attendance.changed` recorded not delivered, and a key created under API-002 alone still reading booleans only.
2. Native restore refuses the key (API-002 archive journey).

### Move-in and portability

#### MOVE-001 — Mindbody studio migration

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-INPUT-003 · *Register:* R-6

**Requirement.** The owner uploads actual Mindbody client, membership/autopay, pricing-option/remaining-session, schedule/booking, attendance and transaction exports, including documented CSV exports and Excel report files with their actual extensions verified, plus a source-labeled waiver/document bundle, or authorizes supported API capture. The adapter maps IDs, plan terms, credits, renewal dates, bookings, payments and signed-waiver provenance without treating missing fields as zero.

**Acceptance.**
1. Use a sanitized real Mindbody export set from a consenting studio, preview per-file mappings and unsupported fields, apply, reconcile every record class and opening value, then book a migrated member and run a shadow renewal.

#### MOVE-002 — WellnessLiving studio migration

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-INPUT-003 · *Register:* R-6

**Requirement.** Owners import actual WellnessLiving client, active pass/membership, attendance, booking, sales/payment and waiver exports using its documented Excel report exports, CSV for reports that actually support it, separately obtained waiver documents and support-supplied material where the product does not expose a bulk file. The adapter names absent categories and reconciles expiry, remaining visits and upcoming auto-pay.

**Acceptance.**
1. Preview a consenting studio's real WellnessLiving files, map plans and families, resolve one unsupported field, apply and compare member/visit/due totals and future reservations before approving cutover.

#### MOVE-003 — Vagaro studio migration

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-INPUT-003 · *Register:* R-6

**Requirement.** Owners move Vagaro customer-list Excel exports, calendar/attendance and membership/package/payment report exports plus signed form documents obtained through the vendor's supported export path. Source IDs, family rights, recurring dates, pack value and consent evidence remain attributable; an unavailable export becomes a named migration exception.

**Acceptance.**
1. Upload a sanitized real Vagaro data package, match clients to packages and contracts, preserve signed forms and reconcile future class seats, remaining sessions and next dues; reject a missing mandatory balance rather than silently invent it.

#### MOVE-004 — Momence studio migration

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-INPUT-003 · *Register:* R-6

**Requirement.** The adapter ingests actual Momence customer/report spreadsheets with format and columns inspected at preview, membership and session-credit details, class/appointment bookings, transactions and individually downloaded signed-waiver PDFs, supplemented by authorized API/support exports when needed; original values and unsupported terms are reviewable.

**Acceptance.**
1. Import a real Momence export package with an intro, frozen plan and credit pack; compare the next bill, credit expiry, waiver provenance and booked class roster and require resolution of unsupported terms.

#### MOVE-005 — Arketa studio migration

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-INPUT-003 · *Register:* R-6

**Requirement.** Owners migrate actual Arketa reporting CSV files and client exports with verified columns and authorized membership, package, booking, payment and signed-waiver data packages or API captures. Livestream and on-demand entitlements and source links retain explicit media-transfer permissions. Date-level attendance is distinguished from imported aggregate totals; a total without dates cannot become invented visits.

**Acceptance.**
1. Move a consenting Arketa studio with in-person, livestream and video memberships, reconcile its members, open credits and recurring dates and verify each migrated member's entitled services without publishing unlicensed media.

#### MOVE-006 — PushPress studio migration

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-INPUT-003 · *Register:* R-6

**Requirement.** Owners import actual PushPress member and plan exports, CSV/Excel transaction reports, class/appointment-credit balances, bookings, attendance and waivers with support-assisted exports as needed. The adapter reconciles external Stripe references and upcoming dues without recharging historical transactions.

**Acceptance.**
1. Import a real PushPress package, including appointment sessions and a failed invoice, compare opening balances and the next billing schedule, then repeat import and verify no duplicated credit or collection.

#### MOVE-007 — Processor-coordinated payment-method transfer

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-INPUT-003 · *Register:* R-7

**Requirement.** The studio coordinates outgoing processor-to-Stripe PCI transfer of eligible card or ACH references through approved provider channels, including the receiving-platform Stripe ticket/request-number path documented by PushPress. Arketa Custom accounts require platform coordination; shared use of Stripe never proves independent dashboard access or transferable mandates. Strap stores only new processor references and transfer evidence, maps payer/mandate identity, and securely asks members to reauthorize when transfer is unsupported; cutover never claims every method portable.

**Acceptance.**
1. Complete a provider-approved transfer rehearsal or controlled transfer, reconcile source customers to destination methods, document unsupported ACH mandates and complete secure re-entry; a dry-run renewal identifies every collectible and blocked account without raw payment data in exports.

#### MOVE-008 — Opening credit and liability reconciliation

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-INPUT-003

**Requirement.** A reviewed migration report reconciles memberships, pack and appointment credits, gift cards, account balances, unpaid invoices and deferred value by member and in aggregate, with source amounts, adjustments and sign-off. Missing balances block approval until explicitly resolved.

**Acceptance.**
1. Migrate a studio with partial packs, appointment credits, gift cards, a debt and an overpayment; compare both ledgers and approve a reasoned adjustment; one unresolved member cannot become a silent zero.

#### MOVE-009 — Historical records waivers and consent

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-006, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-INPUT-003

**Requirement.** Imported attendance, charges, payments, communications and signed waivers retain original dates, actor/source IDs and document evidence. Historical acknowledgments never become new signatures, marketing consent is not inferred and imported records cannot trigger old reminders or charges. API-003 obligation before this capability ships: imported attendance appears in `attendance:read` with source `imported` and its original dates, and an import triggers no `attendance.changed` delivery. Transport and record details are in [Studio mechanisms](docs/contract/MECHANISMS.md#move-009).

**Acceptance.**
1. Import a year of history and waiver PDFs with consent records, inspect continuous member history and reports, and run schedulers; no historical charge or message is replayed and an unverified waiver requires renewal.
2. SCHED-009 obligation before its new door ships: preserve verified/revocable makeup authority, participant-owned adult delegation acceptance, the retained original absence/used visit, explicit credit expiry and one original-linked allocation; no erasure, import or staff grant may create a second makeup credit or expose unrelated child records.

#### MOVE-010 — Rehearsal cutover and safe rollback

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-INPUT-003

**Requirement.** Owners rehearse migration, review source-to-target counts, exceptions and next-period billing in a shadow run, choose an explicit cutover instant and disable old autopay before enabling new collection. Incremental refresh and rollback preserve later native edits and cannot replay messages or charges.

**Acceptance.**
1. Rehearse, refresh changed exports, resolve conflicts and approve cutover; simulate a failed processor setup and roll back untouched work while preserving native bookings, with no simultaneous old/new renewal.

#### MOVE-011 — Usable exit exports and recovery

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-INPUT-003

**Requirement.** Owners export all studio records in documented CSV/JSON with signed waivers and media manifests, stable IDs, relationships, credit/payment histories and source provenance, in addition to the complete native archive. Restoring into another authorized installation pauses billing/delivery until reviewed reconnection. The current owner-only exit download includes every current studio record, credit/payment history, waiver text and acknowledgment, retained import original and journal in documented CSV/JSON with stable IDs and relationship metadata.  Native rer export/import remains the separate guarded archive recovery door.  Future BILL-001–015, MSG-001–009, INT-001–009, CHANNEL-001–004, APP-001–005 and MEM-005 carry portable records/documents and paused studio billing/delivery until reviewed human-owner reconnection before shipping. Every later clause adding a record kind, provider or studio door inherits the same obligation.

**Acceptance.**
1. Download the exit bundle and native archive from a populated studio, reconstruct relationships in a fresh receiver, compare totals and documents and confirm no outbound collection or message occurs before reconnection approval.

### Security custody and operating boundaries

#### SEC-001 — Private records and operational recovery

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-SECRET-001, BASE-BACKUP-001, BASE-INSTALL-001, BASE-DATA-006

**Requirement.** The studio controls its deployment and records. Studio doors use encrypted transport; provider diagnostics identify the failing integration and retain actionable stale or error states. Logs redact member notes and payment secrets, and studio exports use the separately granted export right.

**Acceptance.**
1. Deploy to a controlled studio account, exercise role-separated exports and provider failure diagnostics, inspect logs for planted secrets and restore a backup to a recovery installation.

#### SEC-002 — Durable staff and automation audit

*Tier:* Core · *Cites:* BASE-ROLE-001, BASE-ACCESS-002, BASE-AUDIT-001, BASE-DATA-001, BASE-DATA-006, BASE-MAIL-001, BASE-AGENT-005, BASE-AGENT-007, BASE-AGENT-008, BASE-SECRET-001 · *Rules:* D-2, D-1

**Requirement.** Record actor, role, policy/version, time, source and before/after effect for staff access, member changes, billing attempts, autopay skips and postponements, staff-made contract freezes, freeze ends, terminations and canceled terminations with their type or reason code and comment, cash drawer adjustments, refunds, entitlements, attendance corrections, imports, exports, messages and agent execution. Every staff action on a member's behalf through a STAFF-006 staff door is recorded as staff-made with the acting staff member, role, STAFF-001 right used, member, channel (desk, phone, inbox or staff device) and any reason: bookings, cancellations with their early or late type, fee waiver and notice suppression, moves, restores, waitlist actions, spot and guest bookings, makeups, check-ins, sales and card-on-file charges, invoices and receipts sent, payment collections, retries and payment-method changes, plan changes, freezes and terminations, signing sessions launched, and profile, household, intake, preference, referral and privacy-request records. The member/staff pair follows D-1. Studio audit filters include the named staff right and studio action; financial audit evidence follows SEC-003's permitted pseudonymous retention classes.

**Acceptance.**
1. Change staff rights, correct attendance, run a renewal and approved refund, export and erase a member; inspect the complete ordered audit and verify agents and staff cannot rewrite it.
2. As front desk, cancel one member's booking late, early-cancel another with a waiver reason, move and restore bookings and charge a card on file; filtering the audit by member and by right shows each action once as staff-made with its right, channel and reason.
3. SCHED-014 obligation before this capability ships: SCHED-014 staff-made action records in searchable and exportable durable audit history; assert the inherited behavior through its public doors.
4. studio search/export the native owner filters existing canonical staff-made history by exact member, acting staff member, right, channel and command and inclusive studio-local from/through dates.
5. Newest UTC event comes first, with event identity as the tie-breaker; the screen, JSON and CSV select the same records.
6. JSON/CSV preserve event identity, time, actor and role, member, command/action, named right, channel, reason and before/after facts; replay payloads and command results are excluded.
